# VIZION audit capstone — Stage 1 ledger

> **Addendum 2026-08-01 (post-Gate, PR #73):** four entries added at the end of
> this file — `PRV-010` (resolved), `A11Y-012`, `SEC-011`, `DSN-022` — from the
> owner-directed Gemini fix and owner-console work. Headline counts below
> describe the original Stage 1 sweep and exclude the addendum.

Audit date: 2026-08-01 · Repo at `34a56db` (audit docs added on top) · Version 0.3.0

Method: 19 read-only track auditors (multi-agent), each finding carrying
file:line evidence; every S0/S1 candidate then adversarially verified by an
independent skeptic agent (verdicts recorded per entry; two candidates were
downgraded). Cross-track duplicates merged into one canonical entry each
(the canonical entry names its independent co-finders). Pass evidence is
recorded per track and in the invariant table — passing invariants are
evidence rows here, not silence.

## Invariant conformance (INV-01 … INV-14)

| INV-01 | **pass** | All six modes present and consistent: src/lib/constants.ts:9-21 MODES = clarify/polish/expand/condense/reformat/target; src/lib/enhance/modes.ts:9-36 MODE_INSTRUCTIONS + MODE_BLURB cover all six as Record<ModeId,...> (compile-enforced, typecheck_exit=0); src/lib/providers/formatters.ts:181-215 buildSystemPrompt + 297-347 parseEnhancePayload define the one envelope (output required-first, rationale coerced, assumptions/questions/targetNotes/title optional-tolerant); route validates mode against MODES (src/app/api/enhance/route.ts:36,104) and gates knobs per mode; DB enum has all six (20260613072034:3-4 five original + 20260701200716:13 adds polish); SHAPE_PRESERVING={polish,clarify} (formatters.ts:59); auto-target enumerates all six (src/lib/enhance/auto-target.ts:48-53); envelope shape consistent across EnhancePayload (formatters.ts:217-234), EnhanceOutput (adapter.ts:47-64), EnhanceResult (stream-events.ts:27-52), and the route done event (route.ts:302-325). Consumers (ModeRig, EnhanceComposer, TransformationDiff, LibraryBrowser, PromptDetail, DraftsList, templates, export, stores/ui) all key off ModeId; 976/976 unit tests pass incl. tests/unit/formatters.test.ts. |
| INV-02 | **pass** | Every key read sits behind import "server-only" (src/lib/providers/{anthropic,openai,openai-compat,google,mistral,xai,vision,adapter}.ts line 1; src/lib/supabase/admin.ts line 1). Provider SDK imports (@anthropic-ai/sdk, openai) exist only in those server-only modules. src/lib/providers/config.ts exposes key PRESENCE as Boolean only (config.ts:195-197). grep NEXT_PUBLIC_ across src/ shows only SUPABASE_URL, SUPABASE_ANON_KEY, MEDIA_EXTRACTION, APP_VERSION — no key misuse. .env.example documents all 12 provider keys as server-only with public vars separated. Built bundle scan: grep of .next/static/ for sk-ant\|sk-proj\|AIza[0-9A-Za-z_-]{10} and for ANTHROPIC_API_KEY\|MISTRAL_API_KEY\|DASHSCOPE_API_KEY\|SERVICE_ROLE returned zero hits; only NEXT_PUBLIC_MEDIA_EXTRACTION appears in a client chunk (expected public flag). One S3 hygiene gap filed: config.ts itself lacks the server-only guard. |
| INV-03 | **pass** | Both routes reserve before any upstream call: /api/enhance calls reserveSpend at route.ts:211 before enhanceStream at :263; /api/media calls reserveSpend at route.ts:140 before describeImage at :155, one hold covering the single cross-provider vision retry (:162-174). spend_reserve (20260730203737:43-74) takes admission + hold under pg_advisory_xact_lock with rate_limited/cap_reached raises; spend_settle writes the ledger unconditionally; grants are authenticated-only with anon/public revoked (20260730203605:231-243); usage_reservations is RLS default-deny + revoke (:56-57). No unmetered path: only these two routes import provider modules (grep of src/ shows adapter imported solely by enhance route, vision solely by media route); the on-device media path (NEXT_PUBLIC_MEDIA_EXTRACTION=ondevice, AttachmentTray.tsx:55) runs in-browser with no provider key; vision fallback resolution before reservation makes no network call (env presence checks only, vision.ts:260,284). Abort/failed runs still settle estimated usage (route.ts:354-374) or release the hold (route.ts:390, media:178). |
| INV-04 | **partial** | Main path is genuine accounting: all six adapters extract provider-reported usage (anthropic.ts:67-76 message_start/delta snapshots; openai.ts:45,52-57 stream_options.include_usage; openai-compat.ts:122-128; google.ts:105-113 usageMetadata incl. thoughtsTokenCount; mistral.ts:48-53; xai.ts:46,53-58); the route computes costUsd via computeCost from those counts (route.ts:285, adapter.ts:175) and the client marks usage authoritative only on the usage event (use-enhance.ts:172-179); StreamProgress withholds cost until costUsd>0 (StreamProgress.tsx:45) and mid-stream tokenOut estimation is explicitly documented non-authoritative (use-enhance.ts:57-59). VIOLATION: when a provider never reports usage, adapter.ts:146-147 substitutes ~4 chars/token estimates that flow unmarked into the displayed cost (TransformationDiff.tsx:613-614 renders $X.XXXX) and the ledger; openai-compat.ts:21-23 documents this as an expected path. Media route displays $0.0000 when vision usage is absent (vision.ts:132-133 '?? 0'). |

against the current tree, not the changelog. **No regressions were found** —
every previously-resolved item remains resolved; the flagged rows below are
still-open or partial carry-forwards.

| SAFE-01 | resolved | Outbox items carry userId and replay is owner-filtered, skipping (not deleting) other accounts' items: src/lib/pwa/outbox.ts:24,47-54; enqueue stamps userId: outbox.ts:130-136. Persisted UI state remembers its account and drops editorDraft on account change: src/stores/ui.ts:85,218-224 + src/components/ProfileHydrator.tsx:27-40. Cache Storage leak closed by CACHE-01 (navigations NetworkOnly, sw-src.js:114). Cross-account no-replay pinned by tests/unit/security.test.ts:107. Sign-out route still clears cookies only (src/app/auth/sign-out/route.ts:5-11) but every leak path the ledger named (draft, outbox write, cached HTML) is closed by scoping. |

## Track INV

> INV-01/02/03/05 hold cleanly at v0.3.0 (baseline: lint/typecheck/test/build/audit all exit 0, 976 unit tests green): the six-mode contract is complete and compile-enforced end to end, provider keys are statically and bundle-verified server-side, both model routes take an atomic spend_reserve hold before any upstream call with no unmetered provider path, and all ten tables carry RLS from creation with per-operation policies plus default-deny reservations and a server-only service-role path. Three invariants show drift: INV-04 is partial (a documented no-usage fallback presents ~4 chars/token estimates as exact displayed/ledgered cost with no marker; the media route displays $0.0000 on absent vision usage), INV-06 fails (17 rendered UI sites still use emoji-range glyphs U+26A0/2713/2715/270E/2605/2726 — the unfinished remainder of prior ledger item POLISH-01, whose acceptance criterion was zero emoji codepoints in UI source), and INV-07 is partial (the text-on-laser law held everywhere — 0 text-laser uses, every bg-laser text site carries --on-laser at 15.77:1, --accent-ink splits verified at 5.07-6.34:1 on light — but two laser strokes in globals.css render at 1.06:1 on the light page, making the streaming progress sweep effectively invisible in light theme, violating the tokens.css contrast law's own "never a thin stroke on a light surface" clause). Two S3 hygiene items: providers/config.ts lacks a server-only guard, and two applied spend/ledger migrations cross-reference each other by timestamps that do not exist.
>
> Track INV-B verified invariants INV-08 through INV-14 against the live repo with measured evidence (git grep sweeps, sharp-based pixel measurement of all 19 icon PNGs plus the App Router icons, manifest parse, full font-role enumeration, RAF-throttle math review, and per-ruling code checks for PR-1..PR-7). Six of seven invariants pass outright: the icon suite's alpha contract holds exactly as generated (transparent any-matrix, opaque maskable/apple-touch/favicons), the manifest declares both any and maskable, typography roles are correctly scoped (the Footer version line's font-mono is sanctioned by product-spec section 1.3 'version hashes'), the wordmark is plain VIZION with only IO in the theme-aware accent ink, the neural mesh is full-bleed with a genuine 30fps cap, live reduced-motion fallback and hidden-tab pause, and all seven platform rulings remain implemented without drift. The single invariant violation is INV-08: SECURITY.md:5 still routes vulnerability reports to sean@vasey.audio — the unresolved GOV-01 carry-forward, the only VASEY.AUDIO crossover left in tracked content (the footer's Vasey Multimedia entity is R7-sanctioned and distinct). Three hygiene/coverage findings accompany it: the type-scoping test cannot catch the font-mono utility and covers only 24 of 74 tsx surfaces, the icon alpha contract has no automated guard, and the spec-referenced vizion-brand-lockup.html remains absent (documented; component is canon).
>

### INV-001 — Streaming progress sweep and result shimmer use raw --laser on light surfaces, rendering at 1.06:1 — the progress indicator is effectively invisible in light theme

- severity `S1` · confidence `certain` · fix_class `MANUAL-APPROVE` · design_impact `visual` · invariant_ref `INV-07`
- **evidence:** src/styles/globals.css:902-913 (.result-shimmer::before: 1px linear-gradient stop var(--laser) at opacity 0.6) and :926-939 (.stream-progress-sweep: laser gradient over a --hair track). tokens.css light theme leaves --laser at #b7ff3c (tokens.css:101 comment: laser 'unchanged'). Computed: #b7ff3c vs light page #eef0f4 = 1.06:1, vs white #ffffff = 1.21:1, both far under the 3:1 WCAG 1.4.11 floor for meaningful graphics. tokens.css:5-7 contrast law states --laser 'is never used as text or a thin stroke on a light surface'. The equivalent problem was already solved for every other stroke via --accent-ink (globals.css:123-125, 743; tailwind.config focus ring).
- **blast_radius:** src/styles/globals.css (2 rules); every light-theme streaming surface — StreamProgress renders in EnhanceComposer, PromptDetail re-runs, and AttachmentTray upload progress; result-shimmer on the active result card. Dark theme unaffected.
- **proposed_fix:** In both gradients replace var(--laser) with var(--accent-ink) (and the sweep's box-shadow glow with an accent-ink-derived color-mix). Identical rendering on dark (--accent-ink resolves to --laser there); light theme gets #3f6b00 at 5.55:1 vs page. globals.css is a protected path, hence MANUAL-APPROVE.
- **verification:** python3 contrast check of #3f6b00 vs #eef0f4 (>=3:1 non-text, actual 5.55:1) plus visual pass: run the app in light theme, start an enhancement, confirm the sweep is visible on the track.
- **adversarial verdict:** CONFIRMED — Verified in code and by computation: globals.css:910 and :932 use raw var(--laser) in both gradients with no light-theme override anywhere in the repo; tokens.css:101 leaves --laser at #b7ff3c in light theme; WCAG math gives 1.06:1 vs page #eef0f4, 1.21:1 vs white, and 1.22:1 vs the composited hair track #d3d5d9 — all far under the 3:1 non-text floor, so the sweep is effectively invisible in light theme. This directly violates the LOCKED contrast law at tokens.css:5-7 ("--laser is never used as text or a thin stroke on a light surface"), the exact problem the codebase already fixes elsewhere via --accent-ink (globals.css:123-139 focus ring, :743-771 timeline rail, Footer, NeuralMeshBackground), confirming these two rules were missed rather than exempted. Blast radius checks out (StreamingResult.tsx:26,32; AttachmentTray.tsx:536; TransformationDiff.tsx:290) and the reduced-motion variant at globals.css:950 inherits the same laser gradient. No conflict with PR-1..PR-7, which are all platform/API rulings. Proposed fix verified: #3f6b00 vs #eef0f4 = 5.55:1, and --accent-ink resolves to --laser on dark so dark rendering is unchanged. Sole mitigation: the adjacent step label is AA silver text, so state is not entirely lost — but a true invariant violation floors at S1, so severity stands.
- **independently found by:** DSN ("Raw --laser used as the stream-progress sweep and result-shimmer stroke renders at ~1.1:1 on the light theme, ")
- **disposition:** resolved — Stage 2 W1: both gradients (and the sweep glow) now use var(--accent-ink); dark rendering unchanged, light renders at 5.55:1

### INV-002 — Seventeen rendered UI sites still use emoji-range glyphs (U+26A0, U+2713, U+2715, U+270E, U+2605, U+2726) as icons — the unfinished remainder of prior ledger item POLISH-01

- severity `S1` · confidence `certain` · fix_class `MANUAL-APPROVE` · design_impact `visual` · invariant_ref `INV-06` · prior_ref `POLISH-01`
- **evidence:** python3 unicodedata scan over src/**/*.{ts,tsx,css}: UI hits at EnhanceComposer.tsx:292 (U+26A0 in cap-warning string) and :514 (U+2726 template button); AttachmentTray.tsx:576 (U+2713 '✓ In prompt') and :595 (U+26A0); LibraryBrowser.tsx:213,379,526,570 (U+2605 favorite star, incl. on the bg-laser swipe panel) and :407,545 (U+2715); CollectionSheet.tsx:136 (U+2713 current mark), :150 (U+270E rename), :159 (U+2715 delete); copy/save status glyphs at PartialOutput.tsx:33, TransformationDiff.tsx:362,492,762, GenerateSheet.tsx:108,115, AttachmentDetailsSheet.tsx:141, Field.tsx:71 (U+2713). Prior ledger POLISH-01 ('Replace emoji glyphs', acceptance 'No emoji codepoints in UI source') listed check/warning in its confirmed inventory; tasks/lessons.md 2026-07-30 ('Emoji are platform-colored type, not icons') retired the picture/film/headphone/reset/paperclip glyphs but not these. An SVG replacement system already exists (DeveloperIcon.tsx pattern, ui/CheckGlyph.tsx is a ready-made stroke check). Comment/doc occurrences (12 hits, e.g. AttachmentTray.tsx:35, tokens.css:51) and CHANGELOG.md:104/233/1406 prose are excluded — docs, not UI.
- **blast_radius:** 9 component files across enhance, library, media, and settings routes.
- **proposed_fix:** Replace each glyph with a 1.5px-stroke inline SVG on the 24px grid following DeveloperIcon.tsx / CheckGlyph.tsx (reuse CheckGlyph for the ✓ sites; add star, x, pencil, warning, and spark glyphs). Keep aria-label/aria-hidden semantics unchanged; keep the 'Copied'/'Saved' status text, swapping only the trailing mark.
- **verification:** Re-run the emoji codepoint scan (ranges U+1F300-1FAFF, U+2600-27BF, U+2B00-2BFF, VS16) over src/**/*.{tsx,ts,css}: zero hits outside comments; then npm run lint && npm run typecheck && npx vitest run.
- **adversarial verdict:** CONFIRMED — Reproduced the scan: all cited lines are real rendered-UI glyph sites (e.g. CollectionSheet.tsx:150 renders <span aria-hidden="true">U+270E</span>; EnhanceComposer.tsx:292 interpolates U+26A0 into the cap-warning string), and the excluded hits are genuinely comments. Ledger POLISH-01 (docs/audits/VIZION-enhancement-ledger.json:1561, evaluation.md:1413) confirms title "Replace emoji glyphs", acceptance "No emoji codepoints in UI source", with check and warning in its inventory; tasks/lessons.md:2039-2064 (2026-07-30) retired only the picture/film/headphone/reset/paperclip glyphs; DeveloperIcon.tsx and ui/CheckGlyph.tsx exist as claimed; PR-1..PR-7 concern platform APIs and taxonomy, so no settled ruling is contradicted. One arithmetic error in the claim's favor: the evidence enumerates 21 rendered sites across the 9 files, not 17 — the finding undercounts. S1 stands under the stated rule that a true invariant violation is minimum S1 (I cannot independently read INV-06's text, which lives in the audit prompt, but every checkable fact holds).
- **disposition:** resolved — Stage 2 W1: all 21 rendered emoji sites (plus the two adjacent ▤/⌂ dingbats in the same menu) replaced with the shared SVG glyph language (src/components/ui/glyphs.tsx); codepoint scan over src/ is clean outside comments

### INV-003 — SECURITY.md still directs vulnerability reports to sean@vasey.audio, a VASEY.AUDIO address in public repo metadata of a VASEY/AI product (carry-forward of GOV-01, unresolved).

- severity `S1` · confidence `certain` · fix_class `MANUAL-APPROVE` · design_impact `none` · invariant_ref `INV-08` · prior_ref `GOV-01 (v0.2.1 ledger /proposals/61, VIZION-enhancement-ledger.json:1982-1989)`
- **evidence:** SECURITY.md:5 'Please report security issues privately to **sean@vasey.audio**'. CLAUDE.md section 6: 'Brand separation: VASEY/AI only. Zero VASEY.AUDIO crossover in copy, assets, or metadata.' Prior audit flagged it (docs/audits/VIZION-enhancement-evaluation.md:1357-1368, acceptance GOV-01 at line 1525); grep of CHANGELOG.md and tasks/lessons.md shows no resolution entry. All other tracked surfaces are clean: git grep -i 'vasey[ ._-]?audio' hits only the rule statements (CLAUDE.md:84, README.md:105, plan/spec docs) and the audit record itself; package.json:5, public/manifest.webmanifest:4, and src/ carry VASEY/AI only. The footer's 'VASEY Multimedia' (src/components/Footer.tsx:49,105,110 -> vaseymultimedia.com) is a distinct entity, sanctioned by the R7 remediation entry (CHANGELOG.md:2407-2410 'canonical Footer ... VASEY/AI Presents / Vasey Multimedia') and tasks/lessons.md:241-246 — it is not VASEY.AUDIO crossover.
- **blast_radius:** SECURITY.md only; externally visible because the repository is public. No route or runtime impact.
- **proposed_fix:** Execute the already-adjudicated GOV-01 plan: enable GitHub Private Vulnerability Reporting (human repo-settings action), then edit SECURITY.md:5 to point at it; per GOV-01 risk note, do not remove the existing contact until the replacement is live. Creating a VASEY/AI mailbox remains an explicitly human task.
- **verification:** grep -ci 'vasey.audio' SECURITY.md returns 0 after the edit, and the GitHub repo shows Private Vulnerability Reporting enabled.
- **adversarial verdict:** CONFIRMED — SECURITY.md:5 verbatim contains 'sean@vasey.audio' at HEAD (a4072e6, 2026-08-01), directly violating CLAUDE.md:84 'Zero VASEY.AUDIO crossover in copy, assets, or metadata' (INV-08). Carry-forward verified: GOV-01 confirmed in docs/audits/VIZION-enhancement-evaluation.md:1357-1368 (acceptance at 1525) and ledger lines 1978-2007 ('P1 - implement next'), with git log of SECURITY.md showing no fix commit and no resolution entry in CHANGELOG.md or tasks/lessons.md. Public visibility is evidenced in the ledger ('githubRepoVisibility: public'). Repo-wide grep confirms this is the only live violation; the Footer's 'Vasey Multimedia' is a distinct sanctioned entity per CHANGELOG R7. No PR-1..PR-7 ruling touches brand separation. S1 is the contract minimum for a true invariant violation and the blast radius is correctly scoped, so severity is not inflated.
- **independently found by:** PRI ("SECURITY.md still routes vulnerability reports to sean@vasey.audio, violating the section 6 zero-VASEY.AUDIO-c"); DOC ("SECURITY.md still directs vulnerability reports to sean@vasey.audio, a VASEY.AUDIO address on a public VASEY/A")
- **disposition:** resolved — Stage 2 W1: SECURITY.md routes reports to GitHub private vulnerability reporting; owner action outstanding: enable PVR in repo Settings → Advanced Security (until then the link 404s — no VASEY.AUDIO address remains either way)

### INV-004 — R2 partial: the footer version line renders in JetBrains Mono (font-mono) outside the enhanced-prompt output region, and Footer.tsx is not covered by the type-scoping guard test.

- severity `S1` · confidence `certain` · fix_class `MANUAL-APPROVE` · design_impact `visual` · invariant_ref `INV-11`
- **evidence:** src/components/Footer.tsx:99 className 'font-mono mb-4 text-[11px] tabular-nums text-silver' on 'VIZ(IO)N v{APP_VERSION} ...'; tailwind.config.ts:48-52 maps font-mono to var(--font-mono) = JetBrains (tokens.css:64-66). The recorded gate (CHANGELOG.md:2384-2386) says JetBrains is 'scoped to the enhanced-prompt output/result region ONLY ... guarded by tests/unit/type-scoping.test.ts', but that test's UI_ONLY_FILES list (type-scoping.test.ts:12-37) omits Footer.tsx and its regex targets the standalone 'mono' class, not the 'font-mono' utility. Conflicting in-repo intent: globals.css:556 comments the .mono utility as for 'prompts, diffs, tokens, version hashes' - which would bless the footer usage.
- **blast_radius:** src/components/Footer.tsx (rendered on sign-in, enhance, library, profile); tests/unit/type-scoping.test.ts
- **proposed_fix:** Rule whether version strings are a sanctioned mono surface: if yes, amend the R2 gate wording and extend type-scoping.test.ts to also match font-mono so the widened contract is guarded; if no, change Footer.tsx:99 to font-body and add Footer.tsx to UI_ONLY_FILES.
- **verification:** npx vitest run tests/unit/type-scoping.test.ts after the ruling's edit - the extended regex/file list must fail on reintroduction.
- **adversarial verdict:** CONFIRMED — Lead-verified: src/components/Footer.tsx:99 applies font-mono to the version line, a UI metadata surface outside the enhanced-prompt output region. INV-11 is unambiguous (JetBrains Mono: output region only), so this is a violation, not a ruling question. Secondary genuinely-ambiguous site kept as a sub-question inside this entry: AttachmentDetailsSheet.tsx:101,119 puts the mono class on textareas that edit model-written prose (an editable output region) - whether editable output keeps mono needs a ruling, but the footer version line does not.
- **disposition:** resolved — Stage 2 W1: Footer version line is font-body + tabular-nums; Footer.tsx is inside the rewritten full-surface type-scoping scan (INV-006)

### INV-005 — When a provider stream reports no usage, ~4 chars/token estimates are displayed and ledgered as exact cost with no estimate marker; the media route displays $0.0000 on absent vision usage

- severity `S1` · confidence `likely` · fix_class `MANUAL-APPROVE` · design_impact `visual` · invariant_ref `INV-04`
- **evidence:** src/lib/providers/adapter.ts:146-147 substitutes tokenIn=ceil((system+input)/4), tokenOut=ceil(raw/4) when a provider never emitted usage; the result then carries costUsd=computeCost(...) (adapter.ts:175) with no estimated flag, rendered as exact '{result.tokenIn}→{result.tokenOut} tok · ${result.costUsd.toFixed(4)}' at TransformationDiff.tsx:613-614 and settled to the ledger (route.ts:367-374). openai-compat.ts:21-23 documents this as an expected path ('Providers that report usage do so unprompted in the final chunk; when none arrives the adapter falls back to its ~4 chars/token estimate') — six compat providers cannot request usage via stream_options. Media route: vision.ts:132-133 defaults prompt/completion tokens to 0 when the OpenAI-path response omits usage, so /api/media returns costUsd 0 displayed as actual at AttachmentDetailsSheet.tsx:84-85. Normal paths are genuine (all adapters extract provider-reported counts; the client's mid-stream estimate is gated by usageAuthoritative and cost is withheld until an authoritative usage event, use-enhance.ts:57-59,172-179).
- **blast_radius:** src/lib/providers/adapter.ts, src/lib/providers/vision.ts, src/lib/enhance/stream-events.ts, src/app/api/enhance/route.ts, src/app/api/media/route.ts, src/components/diff/TransformationDiff.tsx, src/components/media/AttachmentDetailsSheet.tsx; routes /api/enhance and /api/media; result and attachment-usage displays.
- **proposed_fix:** Carry a usageEstimated flag on EnhanceOutput/EnhanceResult set when the adapter's fallback (or vision's ?? 0 default) fires; render the cost with an approximation marker (e.g. '~$0.0123') and persist the flag alongside the ledger write. Minimum viable: mark the display; ledger conservatism can stay as-is (it exists for cap integrity).
- **verification:** New unit test: drive enhanceStream with a mock ProviderStream yielding text but no usage chunk; assert result.usageEstimated === true and that the result view renders the marker. npx vitest run tests/unit/<new-test>.
- **adversarial verdict:** CONFIRMED — Every cited line verified: adapter.ts:146-147 substitutes ceil(chars/4) estimates when no usage chunk arrived, adapter.ts:175 prices them via computeCost with no estimate flag on EnhanceOutput (only `salvaged` exists), TransformationDiff.tsx:613-614 renders the result as exact to four decimals, and route.ts:360-374 settles the same figures to the usage_events ledger unmarked. openai-compat.ts:20-23 confirms the path is documented-expected (no stream_options sent, so any strict-OpenAI-semantics compat endpoint omits stream usage every run; seven providers use the factory, not six — immaterial). vision.ts:132-133 (and the Google path at 197-201) default absent usage to 0, so /api/media settles $0 against the cap and AttachmentDetailsSheet.tsx:84-85 displays $0.0000 as actual. The client's mid-stream gating (use-enhance.ts:57-59,172-179; route.ts:279-286) is genuine as claimed — only the final result/ledger path is dishonest. No conflict with PR-1..PR-7 (all iOS/browser-compat/taxonomy). Prior POLISH-03 precedent covered only the transient composer estimate that authoritative data replaces, not the permanent result/ledger record, so it does not cap severity; under the audit rule that a true invariant violation floors at S1 (INV-04 tagged; invariant text lives in the audit prompt, not the repo), S1 holds.
- **disposition:** resolved — Stage 2 W1: usageEstimated rides EnhanceOutput/EnhanceResult and VisionResult; both displays render ≈; spend_settle ledgers an estimated flag (migration 20260801190000, applied); abort-path settles estimated=true; pinned by tests/unit/usage-estimated.test.ts

### INV-006 — The type-scoping guard for INV-11 is not airtight: its regex cannot detect the Tailwind font-mono utility and its UI-only file list omits most UI chrome components, so a JetBrains Mono leak onto chrome would ship green.

- severity `S2` · confidence `certain` · fix_class `AUTO-SAFE` · design_impact `none` · invariant_ref `INV-11`
- **evidence:** tests/unit/type-scoping.test.ts:40 MONO_CLASS = /(["'`\s])mono(["'`\s])/ — the character before 'mono' must be quote/backtick/whitespace, so the token 'font-mono' (hyphen prefix) can never match; src/components/Footer.tsx:99 already uses 'font-mono' and would pass even if Footer were listed. UI_ONLY_FILES (lines 12-37) lists 24 files but the repo has 74 tsx components/pages; omitted UI chrome includes Footer.tsx, ScreenHeader.tsx, BrandPills.tsx, library/DraftsList.tsx, library/DraftsToolbar.tsx, library/LibraryFilterSheet.tsx, media/MediaPreviewSheet.tsx, ui/Segmented.tsx, editor/Horizon.tsx, feedback/StreamProgress.tsx, nav/NewPromptFab.tsx, auth/SetPasswordForm.tsx, avatar-crop/AvatarCropper.tsx, and src/app error.tsx/not-found.tsx/(auth) pages. Current state is nonetheless compliant: every standalone .mono consumer is a sanctioned output region (PromptDetail.tsx:353,517; GenerateSheet.tsx:175; AttachmentDetailsSheet.tsx:101,119,124; StreamingResult.tsx:37; PartialOutput.tsx:26; CompareSheet.tsx:35,43; TransformationDiff.tsx:347,435,737), and the single font-mono at Footer.tsx:99 is the version readout, sanctioned by VIZION-product-spec.md:90 (mono role includes 'version hashes') and globals.css:556 ('prompts, diffs, tokens, version hashes'). npx vitest run tests/unit/type-scoping.test.ts: 23 passed. The test also asserts nothing about Bebas (font-display) scoping.
- **blast_radius:** tests/unit/type-scoping.test.ts only; guards every UI surface against INV-11 regressions.
- **proposed_fix:** Tighten the test: (a) extend the pattern to also catch the standalone 'font-mono' utility; (b) invert the model — allowlist the known mono output files (diff/*, PromptDetail, GenerateSheet, AttachmentDetailsSheet) plus the spec-sanctioned Footer version line, and scan all other files under src/components and src/app for both 'mono' and 'font-mono'; (c) optionally assert font-display appears only on heading/wordmark elements.
- **verification:** npx vitest run tests/unit/type-scoping.test.ts stays green on current sources, and goes red when 'font-mono' is injected into e.g. src/components/nav/BottomNav.tsx in a scratch copy.
- **disposition:** resolved — Stage 2 W1: type-scoping test inverted — every tsx under src/components + src/app is scanned, only the seven output-region files may carry mono, and the pattern now catches font-mono

### INV-007 — src/lib/providers/config.ts lacks the server-only guard despite holding PROVIDER_KEY_ENV and env reads — no build-time fence against a future client import

- severity `S3` · confidence `certain` · fix_class `AUTO-SAFE` · design_impact `none` · invariant_ref `INV-02`
- **evidence:** config.ts has no import "server-only" (line 1 is a type import), while every sibling provider module declares it (grep: 8 provider files + admin.ts). It reads process.env at :36-152,156,196 and maps every provider key name at :178-191. Current value-importers are all server-side (enhance/media routes, server-only provider modules, security/spend.ts used only by routes; errors.ts imports type-only), so INV-02 currently passes — but nothing makes a future client import a build error, unlike the rest of the provider layer. No key value can leak today (isProviderConfigured returns Boolean; dynamic env access is undefined in client bundles), so this is hygiene, not a violation.
- **blast_radius:** src/lib/providers/config.ts only; no runtime change.
- **proposed_fix:** Add import "server-only"; as line 1 of src/lib/providers/config.ts (verified: no client component imports it by value; errors.ts's type-only import is erased).
- **verification:** npm run typecheck && npm run build both exit 0 (build fails loudly if any client path actually imports it).
- **independently found by:** PRV ("config.ts is the only providers module without the server-only guard while exporting server-truth helpers (isP")
- **disposition:** resolved — Stage 2 W1: providers/config.ts imports server-only; build green proves no client path imports it

- **evidence:** supabase/migrations/20260730203605_atomic_spend_reservations.sql:124 says the ambiguity fix lives in '20260730230000_fix_spend_reserve_ambiguity.sql' — the actual file is 20260730203737_fix_spend_reserve_ambiguity.sql. 20260730200124_usage_ledger_integrity.sql:32-33 (and :63) reference '20260730210000_usage_ledger_revoke.sql' — the actual file is 20260730202138_usage_ledger_revoke.sql; 20260730202138's own header depends on the same correct sequencing. The SQL semantics are unaffected; only the comment breadcrumbs mislead anyone auditing apply order.

- **proposed_fix:** Do not edit applied migrations (history is append-only per the repo's own note). Record the filename corrections in docs/runbooks (or the next migration's header) mapping the stale 230000/210000 references to 20260730203737 and 20260730202138.
- **verification:** grep -rn '20260730230000\|20260730210000' supabase/migrations docs/ shows the stale references documented or annotated; no migration file content hash changes.

### INV-009 — No automated assertion enforces the icon alpha contract (transparent any-matrix, opaque maskable/apple-touch/favicons); INV-09 is currently verified only by manual measurement and a regeneration regression would ship undetected.

- severity `S3` · confidence `certain` · fix_class `AUTO-SAFE` · design_impact `none` · invariant_ref `INV-09`
- **evidence:** grep -rn -i 'alpha' across tests/, scripts/, .github/ finds no PNG-alpha check (only the TRANSPARENT constant at scripts/generate-icons.mjs:37 and unrelated color-math tests). This audit's sharp-based measurement of all 19 public/icons PNGs passes today: icon-48..1024 (13 files) hasAlpha=true, minAlpha=0, all four corner pixels alpha=0; maskable-192/512 minAlpha=255 (opaque full-bleed Void); apple-touch-icon.png no alpha channel, fully opaque; favicon-16/32/48 opaque per the documented flatten intent (generate-icons.mjs:126-135); src/app/icon.png and apple-icon.png minAlpha=255. Manifest icon paths all resolve to existing files.
- **blast_radius:** public/icons/** (protected path) plus src/app/icon.png/apple-icon.png; regression surface is any future run of scripts/generate-icons.mjs.
- **proposed_fix:** Add a unit test (sharp is already a dependency) asserting: every icon-*.png has an alpha channel with fully transparent corners; maskable-*.png, apple-touch-icon.png, favicon-*.png, src/app/icon.png and apple-icon.png contain no pixel with alpha < 255. Test-only; touches no protected file.
- **verification:** npx vitest run the new test file: green against the current 19 PNGs, red when pointed at a deliberately flattened icon-192 in scratch.
- **disposition:** resolved — Stage 2 W1: tests/unit/icon-alpha.test.ts asserts transparent corners on the 13-icon any-matrix and full opacity on maskable/apple-touch/favicon/App-Router icons via sharp

> Prior-art reconciliation of 26 assigned v0.2.1 ledger items against the 0.3.0 repo. Overall health is strong: all six P0 release blockers show real remediation verified in code — COST-01 (atomic spend_reserve under a per-user advisory lock, both routes reserve/settle/release), DB-01 (recovered base schema, replay-verified via db:verify), CACHE-01 (navigations NetworkOnly in both sw-src.js and the built public/sw.js), SAFE-01 (userId-scoped outbox with owner-checked replay plus account-scoped editorDraft) fully resolved; SAFE-02 and DIFF-01 partially resolved with concrete residuals (enqueueOutbox still swallows IndexedDB failures behind an unconditional "Queued" badge; the enhance route still runs the unbounded O(n*m) diffWords on the server event loop at line 307 while output ceilings rose to 64k). Of the P1s, DB-02, REV-01, MEDIA-01 and PROD-07 are resolved; META-01, PROD-04, PROD-05 and PROD-06 are untouched and still open; PROD-01 remains open but is gated on the PR-7 approval requirement. P2/P3 UX items split: UX-04/05/06/07 and PROD-03 resolved, UX-01/UX-02/PROD-02 partially, UX-03/UX-08/PROD-08 still open. No assigned item regressed. Seven findings emitted (one S1, five S2, one S3/NEEDS-RULING), all still-open or residual P0/P1 material.

Strong convergence on the Library and Settings tracks: all five LIB items and all four SET items are resolved with verified code (facet-derived filter sheet, content-hash dedupe inside a transactional save RPC, rename/semantic titles/soft-delete-with-Undo, keyset pagination with aggregate version counts, shared diff renderer, sectioned Settings with dirty-gated save, per-control status, and full export/delete-account/retention/media controls). PWA-03 and PWA-05 (drop and paste intake) shipped as prescribed; PWA-02 is superseded by the platform-appropriate ?draft= + Shortcuts recipe. Two P1 items remain open and carry findings: APPLE-01 (ten splash PNGs still shipped, zero apple-touch-startup-image links anywhere — unchanged since the audit) and PWA-01's remainder (enqueueOutbox still swallows failures so the 'Queued — syncs when online' chip can assert safety for a dropped save; the CACHE-01/SAFE-01/doc-drift sub-parts are fixed, the static cache-first tier is not). Four P2/P3 items are untouched with zero code movement: PWA-04 (no Cmd+Enter), PWA-06 (manifest still portrait-locked against WCAG 1.3.4), PWA-08 (single static theme-color), PWA-09 (the decide-not-to-build ADR was never written); APPLE-02 matches the ledger's own leave-alone recommendation. Of the blockers, BLOCKED-5 is fully resolved (verbatim migration recovery plus a production-matched schema fingerprint), BLOCKED-3/4/7 are partially resolved (authed e2e stub and db:verify; atomic spend_reserve pinned by tests; client diff bounded while the server route still calls unbounded diffWords), and BLOCKED-1/2/6/8 still block (no Apple hardware, no axe/VoiceOver coverage, production env unreadable, WebKit SW leg still skipped). No regressions were found among the assigned items.

Notably, EXP-03 (favorites, collections, starter templates) shipped in full despite a "not currently worth developing" verdict — superseded, not resolved. The two headline failures persist: GitHub Actions has still never executed a single run (re-measured 2026-07-30 in docs/runbooks/ci-enablement.md — ci.yml has zero run records ever; every gate remains local-only), and SECURITY.md still routes vulnerability reports to sean@vasey.audio in direct violation of the section 6 brand-separation guardrail. Also still open: workbox runtime deps resolve only as transitives (was P1), no rendered a11y measurement (axe/320px/zoom), CSP unsafe-inline, the broken test:int script, the unused TanStack Query layer, the json-stream seek-window degradation, offline.html theme divergence, and 12 unreferenced icon PNGs. ### PRI-001 — The enhance route still runs the unbounded O(n*m) word diff synchronously on the server event loop, and the worst case has grown since the audit

- severity `S1` · confidence `certain` · fix_class `MANUAL-APPROVE` · design_impact `none` · prior_ref `DIFF-01`
- **evidence:** src/app/api/enhance/route.ts:307 calls diffWords(input, result.output) — the unbounded LCS that materializes an (n+1)x(m+1) number table (src/lib/enhance/diff.ts:35-37) — inside the SSE stream before the done event. Input is bounded only at 20,000 chars (route.ts:35 MAX_INPUT_CHARS); output is bounded only by provider ceilings, which were RAISED to 32k/64k tokens for Anthropic targets after the audit (src/lib/providers/anthropic.ts:35: max_tokens: effort xhigh/max ? 64_000 : 32_000). A 20k-char input (~10k+ whitespace-kept tokens, diff.ts:19-21) against a 64k-token output is on the order of 10^9 table cells. The bounded variant (boundedDiffWords, DIFF_TOKEN_BUDGET=2000, diff.ts:72-85) was built for this exact reason but is consumed only by the client (src/components/library/PromptDetail.tsx:163) — the server path was left on the unbounded function.
- **blast_radius:** src/app/api/enhance/route.ts — every /api/enhance run; a large run blocks or OOMs the serverless invocation mid-stream (the finally-block settle can be skipped on OOM, leaving the spend hold to the 5-minute sweep)
- **proposed_fix:** Replace diffWords with boundedDiffWords at route.ts:307 and make the done event's diff field optional (client already renders plain output when the library diff returns null; TransformationDiff needs the same over-budget fallback). Manual approval because it changes the SSE result contract for oversized outputs.
- **verification:** Unit test asserting the route module imports boundedDiffWords and never diffWords; plus a timing probe: node -e over diff.ts diffing 10k vs 100k whitespace-kept tokens currently exceeds seconds/hundreds of MB, and returns null under the bound. Run: npx vitest run tests/unit/diff.test.ts
- **adversarial verdict:** CONFIRMED — route.ts:307 provably calls unbounded diffWords (bounded variant exists but is client-only per grep); git shows the Anthropic ceiling was raised to 32k/64k in commit 1423e0f at 14:10 on 2026-07-27, hours AFTER the audit ledger (03:08) and the client-only DIFF-01 fix (09:59), so the worst case did grow post-audit; an empirical probe on the actual compiled module measured 6.3 s synchronous + ~770 MB heap at 8k x 16k tokens (128M cells), extrapolating to ~7 GB / 60+ s at the post-raise ~1.2e9-cell worst case — OOM before the done event, skipping the finally-block settle (spend.ts:110 confirms the 5-minute sweep). PR-1..PR-7 rulings cover platform APIs and taxonomy only, none touch diffing, and the repo's own audit rated this exact server path P0 — S1 stands.
- **disposition:** resolved — Stage 2 W2: the route diffs via boundedDiffWords (diff: null over budget); TransformationDiff degrades to plain text with a 'too long to diff' note and hides Compare; provider-policy.test.ts pins the import

## Track MOD

> Track MOD is healthy at the core: all 168 targeted unit tests across formatters, adapter-salvage, clarify-questions, mode-label, migrations, auto-target, stream, use-enhance, length/format rails, refine, mode-rig and stream-handoff pass (baseline suite 976/976 green). PASS evidence gathered: (1) DB enum matches the six modes — enhance_mode created with five values (20260613072034) then 'polish' added AFTER 'clarify' (20260701200716), generated types pin the six-value union (database.types.ts:507) and typecheck binds ModeId to it through library actions' p_mode, with migrations.test.ts enforcing create-before-alter and lexical ordering; (2) all six modes carry instruction + blurb (Record<ModeId,...> totality plus mode-label.test.ts); (3) rail-to-mode mapping is enforced at three consistent layers — composer sends format only in reformat and length only where lengthOptions exists, the route validates legality only, and buildSystemPrompt's knobBlock makes stray knobs inert (format-rail/length-rail 'never leaks' tests); (4) thinking rail is per-target and route-validated against TARGET_THINKING_LEVELS with a 400 for illegal levels; (5) bounds: empty input 400, >20k chars 413 for both input and refine.baseInput, mediaContext capped 4x2000; (6) the 0.3.0 salvage layer is correct and tested — completed-output recovery with honest empty rationale, salvaged flag riding to a truthful result-view line, and the length-stop taxonomy (length/max_tokens/MAX_TOKENS/model_length) matches what every provider adapter actually emits (verified emission points in all six adapter files); (7) streaming contract round-trips across arbitrary chunk boundaries and the client retains partial output on error; (8) Clarify's questions flow is contract-sanctioned (named in its own mode instruction, envelope field gated to clarify, answered-pass framing tested) and Polish's per-change review is the only other mode conditional outside the tables; (9) resolveAutoTarget is total over ModeId so a seventh mode is a compile error there. Eight findings survive: one S1 — refinement passes compose self-contradictory prompts (shorter/detail refines vs the mode instructions and, worse, vs the CRITICAL length-preservation clause that follows them on the default clarify mode), violating the codebase's own no-arguing standard that the format knob honors; three S2 — the locked product spec still defines five modes while code cites it for six, refines silently drop the format/length knobs (needs a ruling on intended carry-forward), and check-model-enum.mjs omits an enhance_mode 'polish' hosted-drift probe despite the migration declaring it deploy-blocking and the script existing for exactly that incident class; plus four S3 hygiene items (abort-path token under-estimate, hardcoded six-mode skeleton/copy, dead 'thinking' wire event, and the prior-ledger json-stream seek-buffer fragility carried forward from PROD-06 as still-open).
>
| id | sev | conf | fix class | design | title |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| MOD-001 | S1 | certain | MANUAL-APPROVE | none | Refinement passes compose self-contradictory system prompts: 'Make shorter'/'More detail' chips on shape-preserving modes are countermanded by the CRITICAL length-preservation clause that follows them, and detail/shorter refines contradict the Polish/Condense mode instructions with no supersedence clause |
| MOD-002 | S2 | certain | MANUAL-APPROVE | none | The locked product spec still defines FIVE modes and a five-value enum, while code comments cite it as the authority for SIX — a live mode-count assumption in an authoritative companion document |
| MOD-003 | S2 | certain | NEEDS-RULING | none | Refinement passes silently drop the format and length knobs: a Reformat run with an explicitly chosen shape regains the 'whichever fits' latitude on refine, and Condense/Expand depth settings vanish |
| MOD-004 | S2 | certain | AUTO-REVIEW | none | scripts/check-model-enum.mjs never probes the hosted enhance_mode enum for 'polish' — the exact committed-but-unapplied drift class the script exists for, and the polish migration's own header declares deploy-blocking |
| MOD-005 | S3 | certain | NEEDS-RULING | visual | ModeRig is a six-cell instrument while the R5 gate ledger records five modes, and the recorded five-cell arrangement claims (symmetry breakpoints, centered picker pill) no longer describe the shipped control - a ruling is needed on which is authoritative. |
| MOD-006 | S3 | certain | AUTO-REVIEW | none | Abort-path token estimate in the enhance route omits the system prompt (and media context), under-counting spend against the daily cap relative to the adapter's own estimator |

### MOD-001 — Refinement passes compose self-contradictory system prompts: 'Make shorter'/'More detail' chips on shape-preserving modes are countermanded by the CRITICAL length-preservation clause that follows them, and detail/shorter refines contradict the Polish/Condense mode instructions with no supersedence clause

- severity `S1` · confidence `certain` · fix_class `MANUAL-APPROVE` · design_impact `none`
- **evidence:** Composition order in buildSystemPrompt (src/lib/providers/formatters.ts:188-197): MODE_INSTRUCTIONS -> knobBlock -> refineBlock -> conventions -> outputContract. For polish/clarify, conventions = FORMAT_PRESERVATION (formatters.ts:112-113) which reads 'OUTPUT SHAPE — CRITICAL: ... Preserve the input's existing format, voice, and length' and is emitted AFTER the refine block. REFINE_INSTRUCTIONS.shorter (formatters.ts:84-85) says 'Make it meaningfully shorter'; REFINE_INSTRUCTIONS.detail (86-87) says 'Add depth — concrete constraints, examples, and acceptance criteria'. So clarify (the DEFAULT mode, stores/ui.ts:138) + 'Make shorter' produces a prompt whose last standing shape rule orders length preservation. Additionally MODE_INSTRUCTIONS.polish (src/lib/enhance/modes.ts:11) 'Do NOT add, remove, reorder, or elaborate on ideas' contradicts both detail ('Add depth') and shorter ('remove' is required to shorten); MODE_INSTRUCTIONS.condense (modes.ts:16-17) 'Strip redundancy to the minimum viable prompt' contradicts detail. The chips are offered unconditionally for all six modes (src/components/diff/TransformationDiff.tsx:42-46, 376-394; onRefine always passed at src/components/editor/EnhanceComposer.tsx:639). The codebase's own standard forbids this: the format knob explicitly withdraws conflicting latitude ('This shape is chosen — it replaces the "whichever fits" latitude above', formatters.ts:165) and tasks/lessons.md:975-978 states a prompt must never 'argue with itself'. No test covers refine-instruction/mode-instruction consistency (refine.test.tsx pins wiring only; formatters.test.ts:109-134 pins presence, not non-contradiction).
- **blast_radius:** src/lib/providers/formatters.ts (REFINE_INSTRUCTIONS, refineBlock, buildSystemPrompt); every refinement request through /api/enhance for all 16 targets; worst on clarify/polish results (clarify is the default mode) where the 'Make shorter' chip's instruction is overruled by a later CRITICAL-flagged clause
- **proposed_fix:** Give refineBlock the same supersedence treatment the format knob already has: append a clause to each refine instruction stating it scopes/overrides the mode instruction's change limits and (for shorter/detail on shape-preserving modes) the FORMAT_PRESERVATION length clause for this pass only — e.g. 'This pass supersedes any earlier instruction that forbids the change it names; all other constraints stand.' Protected path, so wording needs owner approval.
- **verification:** Unit test: for each RefineKind x ModeId, assert the built prompt does not simultaneously contain 'Make it meaningfully shorter' and 'Preserve the input's existing format, voice, and length' without a supersedence sentence between/after them; plus a semantic spot-check (PROD-04 harness pattern) that clarify+shorter actually shortens.
- **adversarial verdict:** CONFIRMED — Executed the real buildSystemPrompt via tsx: clarify (persisted default, stores/ui.ts:138) + "Make shorter" yields "Make it meaningfully shorter" (offset 773) followed by "OUTPUT SHAPE — CRITICAL: ... Preserve the input's existing format, voice, and length" (offsets 888/1015) with no supersedence clause anywhere after the refine instruction (the only "replaces" hit is the unrelated questions clause); polish+detail simultaneously contains "Do NOT add, remove, reorder, or elaborate on ideas" and "Add depth". Reachability verified: handleRefine re-sends the same mode (EnhanceComposer.tsx:243) and chips render for all modes (onRefine always passed, line 639). The repo's own standard treats a weaker conflict as needing explicit supersedence (formatters.ts:165) and lessons.md names "a prompt that argues with itself" as the guarded defect class; no test asserts non-contradiction. No conflict with PR-1..PR-7 (platform rulings cover unrelated topics). S1 holds: default-mode paid refine path for all 16 targets composes a self-contradictory system prompt whose last CRITICAL-flagged clause countermands the user's clicked action.
- **disposition:** resolved — Stage 2 W2: shorter/detail refine instructions carry an explicit supersedence clause and FORMAT_PRESERVATION cedes its length clause to those passes; pinned by formatters.test.ts non-contradiction cases

### MOD-002 — The locked product spec still defines FIVE modes and a five-value enum, while code comments cite it as the authority for SIX — a live mode-count assumption in an authoritative companion document

- severity `S2` · confidence `certain` · fix_class `MANUAL-APPROVE` · design_impact `none`
- **evidence:** VIZION-product-spec.md:188-196 (§4.1 table) lists Clarify, Expand, Condense, Reformat, Model-Targeted Adaptation — no Polish; VIZION-product-spec.md:269 sketches mode enum('clarify','expand','condense','reformat','target') — five values, while the real DB enum has six (supabase/migrations/20260613072034 + 20260701200716; src/lib/supabase/database.types.ts:507). Code cites the spec for the six: src/lib/enhance/modes.ts:4 'The six enhancement modes (product-spec §4.1)', src/lib/constants.ts:6, and TransformationDiff.tsx:49-52 cites '§1.1, §4.1' for Polish per-change accept/reject, which appears nowhere in the spec (grep for 'polish'/'per-change' in the spec returns nothing). CLAUDE.md declares the spec an authoritative companion 'treat as locked'.
- **blast_radius:** VIZION-product-spec.md; any agent or human using the spec as source of truth will build five-mode assumptions; misciting comments in src/lib/enhance/modes.ts, src/lib/constants.ts, src/components/diff/TransformationDiff.tsx
- **proposed_fix:** With owner approval (the spec is locked): add Polish to the §4.1 table and 'polish' to the enum sketch at spec line 269, and record per-change review under Polish; or, if the spec must not change, correct the three code comments to cite the migration/UX-audit rather than §4.1. PR-7 (taxonomy immutable) is not contravened — Polish already shipped; this reconciles the record, it does not change the taxonomy.
- **verification:** grep -n 'polish' VIZION-product-spec.md returns the mode table row and the enum sketch; grep -c mode rows in §4.1 equals MODES.length.
- **disposition:** resolved — Stage 2 W5 (ruling Q2=a): the locked-spec five-mode text is reclassified historical, not rewritten — the three v1 canon files moved to docs/history/ and ADR-0005 names code+CHANGELOG+tokens.css+ledger as the living canon (six modes). = DOC-005

### MOD-003 — Refinement passes silently drop the format and length knobs: a Reformat run with an explicitly chosen shape regains the 'whichever fits' latitude on refine, and Condense/Expand depth settings vanish

- severity `S2` · confidence `certain` · fix_class `NEEDS-RULING` · design_impact `none`
- **evidence:** src/components/editor/EnhanceComposer.tsx:240-253 (handleRefine) sends only input/mode/target/thinkingLevel/refine — no format, no length — while runEnhance (166-169) sends both; formatters.ts knobBlock (152-169) therefore emits nothing on the refine pass, so for mode=reformat the mode instruction's 'whichever best fits the task' offer (modes.ts:18-19) is back in force even though the user withdrew it ('This shape is chosen — it replaces the "whichever fits" latitude', formatters.ts:165) on the pass that produced the input. A condense run at 'Essential' refined with 'More detail' loses its depth dial entirely. refine.test.tsx:77-106 pins kind/baseInput/target only; no test states the intended knob behavior either way.
- **blast_radius:** src/components/editor/EnhanceComposer.tsx handleRefine; refinement quality for reformat/condense/expand runs (3 of 6 modes); no data or billing impact
- **proposed_fix:** Ruling needed on which knobs carry into which refine kinds: format should plausibly persist on every refine of a reformat run (the shape was an explicit choice), but re-sending length into a 'shorter' refine would itself create a contradiction (e.g. Expand 'COMPREHENSIVE' + 'make shorter'). Minimum viable change once ruled: carry format in handleRefine for reformat; document length as deliberately dropped.
- **verification:** After ruling: unit test in refine.test.tsx asserting mutate call [1] carries format='xml' when the first run was reformat+xml (and asserting the ruled length behavior).
- **disposition:** resolved — Stage 2 W2 (ruling Q4=a): the run's format/length snapshot rides view.submitted and re-sends on every refine and answered pass; refine.test.tsx pins the carry

### MOD-004 — scripts/check-model-enum.mjs never probes the hosted enhance_mode enum for 'polish' — the exact committed-but-unapplied drift class the script exists for, and the polish migration's own header declares deploy-blocking

- severity `S2` · confidence `certain` · fix_class `AUTO-REVIEW` · design_impact `none`
- **evidence:** scripts/check-model-enum.mjs probes model_target labels (probe(), lines 111-121), migrated columns (COLUMN_PROBES, 63-91), and RPC functions (FUNCTION_PROBES, 93-109); grep for 'enhance_mode' in scripts/ returns nothing. supabase/migrations/20260701200716_add_polish_enhance_mode.sql:6-8 states 'REQUIRED before deploying the branch that adds Polish: without this value, saving a polished prompt version ... is rejected by the enum constraint.' The script's header (lines 8-13) records that this drift class already shipped once (Luna/Terra/Kimi/MiniMax migration committed but never applied, every DB write 22P02 while CI stayed green). A prompts.current_mode column probe exists (COLUMN_PROBES) but a column probe cannot detect a missing enum VALUE.
- **blast_radius:** scripts/check-model-enum.mjs preflight coverage; on a drifted host, every Polish save/version write and Polish usage-ledger settle fails with Postgres 22P02 undetected by the preflight
- **proposed_fix:** Add an enum-value probe using the same read-only PostgREST cast trick: GET /rest/v1/prompts?select=id&limit=0&current_mode=eq.polish — 200 = present, 400 matching /invalid input value for enum enhance_mode/ = missing; wire it into the existing drift reporting citing 20260701200716.
- **verification:** node scripts/check-model-enum.mjs against a project without the polish migration exits 1 naming enhance_mode; against a current project prints a new check-pass line.
- **disposition:** resolved — Stage 2 W2: check-model-enum.mjs probes hosted enhance_mode for 'polish' via the current_mode cast trick, wired into the drift report

### MOD-005 — ModeRig is a six-cell instrument while the R5 gate ledger records five modes, and the recorded five-cell arrangement claims (symmetry breakpoints, centered picker pill) no longer describe the shipped control - a ruling is needed on which is authoritative.

- severity `S3` · confidence `certain` · fix_class `NEEDS-RULING` · design_impact `visual`
- **evidence:** Facts, no redesign proposed. (1) Cell count: src/lib/constants.ts:9-20 defines SIX modes - clarify, polish, expand, condense, reformat, target(label 'Adapt'); Polish sits at index 1 (second cell) with a pencil icon (ModeRig.tsx:172-177); 'target' renders as 'Adapt' (constants.ts:19, renamed by the 2026-07 UX audit, id preserved for DB enum/wire compat). (2) Grid geometry: ModeRig.tsx:51 'grid grid-cols-6 gap-0 rounded-2xl p-1' = Tailwind 3.4 repeat(6, minmax(0,1fr)) - six equal tracks at every width by construction. (3) Indicator math: ModeRig.tsx:83-90 - absolute span, inset-y-1 left-1, width calc((100% - 0.5rem)/6), translateX(activeIndex * 100%); since the containing block is the padding box and p-1 = 4px/side, (padding-box - 8px)/6 equals the content-box track width exactly - the geometry was RE-DERIVED FOR SIX cells and does NOT assume five; keyboard wrap also uses MODES.length (line 57). (4) Ledger conflict: CHANGELOG.md:2399-2400 records R5 as 'the FIVE modes are now one glass chassis (ModeRig) ... symmetric at 360/390/430px', while ModeRig.tsx:8 documents 'six equal cells (grid repeat(6,1fr))'; the Polish addition (CHANGELOG.md:2283-2291) post-dates the remediation entry but the R5 record was never amended. (5) Overflow at 320px: measured from the vendored RedditSans-500.woff2 (fontTools advance widths, 11px + tracking-wide 0.025em): Condense 51.9px, Reformat 49.1px vs a 46.7px cell ((320-32-8)/6); the cell buttons (ModeRig.tsx:110-118) have no truncate/overflow-hidden/wrap handling, so both labels paint ~5px past their cell bounds toward each other at 320px; at 360px the cell is 53.3px and Condense fits with <1px margin. (6) The R5 clause 'target-model picker is a centered content-width pill' is superseded by the documented integrated composer (CHANGELOG.md:2363-2368, picker nested in the composer top rail: EnhanceComposer.tsx:345-347). (7) No automated geometry test covers the rig at 360/390/430 or 320 (mode-rig.test.tsx is roles/keys only; authed.spec.ts:171 tests only the Horizon band).
- **blast_radius:** src/components/editor/ModeRig.tsx, src/lib/constants.ts, src/app/(app)/enhance/page.tsx (enhance route), CHANGELOG.md R5 record; visually affects the enhance screen at <=360px
- **proposed_fix:** Ruling needed on two coupled questions: (a) is the six-cell single-row arrangement the blessed successor to the recorded five-cell R5 gate (amend the gate record), or must the sixth mode be presented differently; (b) how should the 320px label overflow be resolved (smaller/condensed label type, abbreviations, two-row layout, or accepting 360px as the floor). No change should be made before the ruling.
- **verification:** After ruling: Playwright viewport sweep at 320/360/390/430 asserting each radio cell's boundingBox width equals (rig content width)/6 and that each label span's scrollWidth <= its cell width; plus a gate-ledger note amending CHANGELOG.md R5.
- **adversarial verdict:** DOWNGRADED — All cited facts verify (six modes in constants.ts:9-20, grid-cols-6 at ModeRig.tsx:51, R5 five-mode text at CHANGELOG.md:2398-2400, picker in composer rail at EnhanceComposer.tsx:345-347; I reproduced the font arithmetic exactly: Condense 51.9px / Reformat 49.1px vs 46.7px cell at 320px). But the S1 'ruling needed on which is authoritative' is manufactured: CHANGELOG is an append-only historical ledger whose newer entries explicitly document the transition (CHANGELOG.md:2289 'now six equal cells', :2278-2281 '5 modes -> 6 modes'), ModeRig.tsx:8 self-documents the six-cell chassis as remediation R5.1, and the settled audit already blesses six as the locked baseline (evaluation :145 'six locked values... unit-tested', ledger UX-02 six-column grid as current state, PR-7 Honored at :215) — so ruling question (a) re-litigates a settled ruling. The surviving defect is a ~5px cosmetic label overflow at 320px only (tap targets unaffected, browser-unmeasured), which the settled audit already flagged verbatim at :1243 as the unmeasured risk, plus missing geometry test coverage. That is S3 documentation/cosmetic polish, not an S1 gate conflict; no invariant is cited or violated.
- **disposition:** pending

### MOD-006 — Abort-path token estimate in the enhance route omits the system prompt (and media context), under-counting spend against the daily cap relative to the adapter's own estimator

- severity `S3` · confidence `certain` · fix_class `AUTO-REVIEW` · design_impact `none`
- **evidence:** src/app/api/enhance/route.ts:358 estimates tokenIn as Math.ceil(input.length / 4) when a client aborts before any provider usage arrives, while the adapter's defensive estimator (src/lib/providers/adapter.ts:146) uses (system.length + input.length) / 4 for the same situation; the route also has providerInput (input + fenced media context, route.ts:228-237) in scope but estimates from bare input. System prompts built by buildSystemPrompt are ~2,000-3,000 chars (~500-750 tokens) per request, so each OpenAI-compat abort-before-usage run under-counts the cap by that share.
- **blast_radius:** src/app/api/enhance/route.ts finally block; daily cost-cap accounting accuracy on aborted runs only; no user-visible behavior
- **proposed_fix:** Estimate from providerInput.length instead of input.length, and add the system-prompt share (either import buildSystemPrompt length or have the adapter expose its estimate); at minimum use providerInput which is already in scope.
- **verification:** Unit test on the route's abort path (mock adapter throwing after first delta with no usage event) asserting settleSpend receives tokenIn >= ceil(providerInput.length/4).
- **disposition:** resolved — Stage 2 W2: the abort-path estimate is ceil((buildSystemPrompt(...)+providerInput)/4), matching the adapter's own estimator

### MOD-007 — Envelope scanner's seek-buffer truncation (field.length + 16) cannot survive pathologically whitespace-padded '"output" :' keys split across chunks — streaming and salvage eligibility degrade while the final parse still succeeds

- severity `S3` · confidence `likely` · fix_class `AUTO-REVIEW` · design_impact `none` · prior_ref `PROD-06`
- **evidence:** src/lib/providers/json-stream.ts:67 — seekBuf = seekBuf.slice(-(field.length + 16)) keeps 22 chars for 'output'; the key regex (line 53) permits unbounded \s* around the colon, so a key emission like '"output"        :        "' exceeding the retained tail across a chunk boundary never matches: no deltas stream, scanner.done stays false, and the adapter's salvage branch (adapter.ts:154-159) becomes unreachable for that run even though parseEnhancePayload on the buffered text still succeeds. Prior audit already recorded this as 'a latent streaming fragility' (docs/audits/VIZION-enhancement-ledger.json, PROD-06 evidence: 'json-stream.ts:67 — seek buffer truncated to field.length+16'). Practical likelihood is low (json_object modes emit compact keys), which is why this is S3 not S2.
- **blast_radius:** src/lib/providers/json-stream.ts; streaming UX and salvage recovery for any provider emitting heavily padded JSON; final result correctness unaffected
- **proposed_fix:** Bound the whitespace in the key regex (e.g. \s{0,8}) to match the retained tail, or retain a larger fixed tail (e.g. field.length + 64); add a stream.test.ts case for a whitespace-padded key split at every chunk boundary.
- **verification:** npx vitest run tests/unit/stream.test.ts with a new case feeding '"output"' + 20 spaces + ':' + spaces + '"hi"' one char per push and asserting full decode.
- **disposition:** resolved — Stage 2 W2: seek tail widened to field.length+64; stream.test.ts feeds a 40-space-padded key at every chunk size

## Track PRV

> Track PRV is structurally healthy: the roster is exactly 16 models across 12 developers and reconciles everywhere it is declared — TARGET_MODELS (src/lib/constants.ts:69-86, 16 ids), TARGETS (src/lib/providers/config.ts:33-153, 16 entries), Provider/Developer unions (12 each, identical string sets), the generated DB types (database.types.ts:509-525, 16 enum values), and the migration-reconstructed model_target enum (7 ALTER..RENAME + 13 ADD VALUE across 20260613..20260726 yields the same 16); all 7 historical renames have LEGACY_TARGET_IDS entries pointing at current ids. Auth handling is uniform (server-only modules, env-only keys, ProviderNotConfiguredError -> pre-stream 503 via isProviderConfigured); error normalization is complete — every adapter and vision path rewraps SDK/fetch failures into ProviderError/ProviderNotConfiguredError and no provider-specific field (prompt_tokens, message_start, reasoning_effort, output_config) appears outside src/lib/providers plus comments; streaming parity holds — all 12 providers stream through 5 implementations into one ProviderStreamChunk grammar, with envelope decoding centralized in the adapter's json-stream scanner and length stops normalized (LENGTH_STOPS covers length/max_tokens/MAX_TOKENS/model_length); vision degrades gracefully via a capability gate plus a keyed cross-provider fallback chain, and both model routes settle or release spend holds in finally blocks. Thinking-depth mapping is per-target and route-validated, with correct no-selector behavior for the seven knob-less targets and correct budget mapping for Qwen. 89/89 targeted provider-track unit tests pass; all baseline gates exit 0. Headline defects: one S1 — the composer sends the pinned target's thinking level on auto-routed requests, so Gemini's 'minimal' (the only level outside the Anthropic auto tiers' ladders) 400s a fully valid UI state; two S2 — timeout/retry policy is absent and non-uniform (Google raw fetch has neither; SDK defaults exceed the route's 60s window, risking stranded spend holds), and the compat-provider fallback token estimate excludes billed reasoning tokens (stripped think spans, DashScope thinking budgets) so the daily cost cap can undercount. Remaining findings are S3 hygiene: duplicated key-env names without a pinning test, 'Opus'-labeled errors for all Anthropic targets, a never-emitted 'thinking' SSE event, three floating model aliases, three self-declared placeholder prices, a missing server-only guard on config.ts, and a speculative OpenAI completion-cap parity gap.
>
| id | sev | conf | fix class | design | title |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| PRV-001 | S1 | certain | AUTO-REVIEW | none | Auto routing rejects a valid composer state: Gemini's 'minimal' thinking level rides an auto-routed request and 400s against the resolved Anthropic target |
| PRV-002 | S2 | certain | AUTO-REVIEW | none | Timeout and retry policy is absent and non-uniform: the Google adapter's raw fetch has no timeout and no retries, while every SDK adapter silently inherits SDK defaults (600s timeout, 2 retries) that exceed the route's 60s window |
| PRV-003 | S2 | likely | AUTO-REVIEW | none | Cost-cap token estimate systematically excludes reasoning tokens for OpenAI-compat providers when stream usage is absent |
| PRV-004 | S3 | certain | AUTO-SAFE | none | Provider key env names are duplicated between PROVIDER_KEY_ENV and per-adapter hardcoded strings with no test pinning the correspondence |
| PRV-005 | S3 | certain | AUTO-REVIEW | none | Anthropic adapter error copy says 'Opus' for all three Anthropic targets, mislabeling Fable 5 and Sonnet 5 failures in user-facing messages |
| PRV-006 | S3 | certain | AUTO-SAFE | none | The 'thinking' SSE event is declared in the wire contract and handled by the client but nothing server-side ever emits it |
| PRV-007 | S3 | likely | MANUAL-APPROVE | none | Three targets ship floating provider aliases (deepseek-chat, mistral-large-latest, qwen-max) so an upstream model swap silently changes behavior and invalidates the target's price table |
| PRV-008 | S3 | likely | MANUAL-APPROVE | none | Kimi K3, MiniMax M3, and GLM-5.2 default prices are self-declared carryovers from prior model series, pending published rates |
| PRV-009 | S3 | speculative | AUTO-REVIEW | none | OpenAI enhance path caps completion at a flat 16k with no per-effort headroom, unlike the Anthropic path which doubles the ceiling for xhigh/max for the same reasons-bill-as-output mechanism |

### PRV-001 — Auto routing rejects a valid composer state: Gemini's 'minimal' thinking level rides an auto-routed request and 400s against the resolved Anthropic target

- severity `S1` · confidence `certain` · fix_class `AUTO-REVIEW` · design_impact `none`
- **evidence:** src/components/editor/EnhanceComposer.tsx:170 sends `...(thinkingLevel ? { thinkingLevel } : {})` unconditionally beside `...(autoTarget ? { auto: true } : {})` (line 165); thinkingLevel is derived from the PINNED targetModel (lines 72-77) and the Thinking rail stays visible under Auto (line 408 gates only on `levelOptions`). src/app/api/enhance/route.ts:122-141 resolves Auto to sonnet_5/opus_5 FIRST (resolveAutoTarget, src/lib/enhance/auto-target.ts:34-36,61-68) then validates thinkingLevel against TARGET_THINKING_LEVELS[resolvedTarget] and returns 400 'That thinking level isn't available for this model.' src/lib/constants.ts:144-150: the Anthropic ladders are [low,medium,high,xhigh,max]; only gemini_3_6_flash carries 'minimal'. auto-target.ts:28-30 claims 'Auto can never land the user on a model whose thinking dial silently disappears' — true for the ladder existing, false for the 'minimal' value. tests/unit/auto-routing-wire.test.tsx has no case combining auto with a thinking level (grep for 'minimal' returns nothing).
- **blast_radius:** src/components/editor/EnhanceComposer.tsx, src/app/api/enhance/route.ts; the /enhance composer UI and POST /api/enhance for any user who pins Gemini 3.6 Flash, selects Thinking=Minimal, then enables Auto
- **proposed_fix:** On an auto-routed request, treat an out-of-ladder thinkingLevel as advisory and drop it instead of 400ing (route: when auto===true and level not in allowedLevels, set thinkingLevel undefined); alternatively the composer omits thinkingLevel whenever auto is on unless the level is valid for both Auto tiers. Route-side drop is the minimum change and also covers stale clients.
- **verification:** Unit test posting {input:'x', mode:'polish', target:'gemini_3_6_flash', auto:true, thinkingLevel:'minimal'} to the route handler must return a 200 SSE stream (currently 400); re-run npx vitest run tests/unit/auto-routing-wire.test.tsx tests/unit/stream.test.ts
- **adversarial verdict:** CONFIRMED — Verified every citation: EnhanceComposer.tsx:72-77 validates thinkingLevel only against the PINNED target's ladder and line 170 sends it beside auto:true (line 165), with the Thinking rail gated solely on levelOptions (line 408) and setAutoTarget (stores/ui.ts:164) clearing nothing; route.ts:122-128 resolves Auto FIRST via resolveAutoTarget (auto-target.ts:34-36 -> sonnet_5/opus_5 only), then lines 133-141 validate against TARGET_THINKING_LEVELS[resolvedTarget] and 400. constants.ts:144-146 shows both Anthropic ladders lack "minimal" and line 150 shows gemini_3_6_flash is the only target with it, so pinned Gemini + Minimal + Auto deterministically 400s ("That thinking level isn't available for this model") on every submit — surfaced as a hard EnhanceError (use-enhance.ts:148-156). Test gap confirmed: auto-routing-wire.test.tsx has auto cases but zero thinkingLevel/minimal coverage. No conflict with PR-1..PR-7 (all WebKit/taxonomy matters). Severity S1 holds: the UI constructs and persists this valid state (thinkingLevels and autoTarget both persist to localStorage), then the product's core action is deterministically blocked with a misleading error naming "this model" when the user chose Auto; borderline S2 given the narrow trigger (one model, one level, Auto opt-in) but not clearly inflated.
- **disposition:** resolved — Stage 2 W2: under auto the route drops an out-of-ladder thinkingLevel (advisory) instead of 400ing a valid composer state

### PRV-002 — Timeout and retry policy is absent and non-uniform: the Google adapter's raw fetch has no timeout and no retries, while every SDK adapter silently inherits SDK defaults (600s timeout, 2 retries) that exceed the route's 60s window

- severity `S2` · confidence `certain` · fix_class `AUTO-REVIEW` · design_impact `none`
- **evidence:** grep -rn 'timeout|maxRetries' src/lib/providers/*.ts returns zero matches. src/lib/providers/google.ts:49 issues fetch() with no AbortSignal or timeout; 0 retries. anthropic.ts:59, openai.ts:29, mistral.ts:28, xai.ts:31, openai-compat.ts:109 construct SDK clients with defaults (Anthropic/OpenAI SDK: 10-minute timeout, 2 retries). src/app/api/enhance/route.ts:41 and src/app/api/media/route.ts:43 set maxDuration=60 — a provider connection that hangs past 60s is killed by the platform, not by the adapter, so the finally block that settles/releases the spend hold (route.ts:347-401) may never run, stranding the hold for the five-minute sweep. Transient Gemini failures get zero retries while identical transients on the other 11 providers are retried twice, invisibly.
- **blast_radius:** All files in src/lib/providers/ that open connections; POST /api/enhance and POST /api/media for all 16 targets, spend-hold hygiene under provider hangs
- **proposed_fix:** Set an explicit sub-maxDuration timeout (e.g. 55_000ms) and an explicit maxRetries on every SDK client construction, and wrap the google.ts fetch with AbortSignal.timeout(55_000); document the uniform policy in one place (config.ts constant).
- **verification:** grep -rn 'timeout|maxRetries|AbortSignal' src/lib/providers/*.ts shows every adapter configured; unit test asserting buildAnthropicParams/compat client options carry the timeout constant
- **disposition:** resolved — Stage 2 W2: PROVIDER_TIMEOUT_MS=55s / PROVIDER_MAX_RETRIES=0 on every SDK client, AbortSignal.timeout on both raw Gemini fetches; policy documented in config.ts, pinned by provider-policy.test.ts

### PRV-003 — Cost-cap token estimate systematically excludes reasoning tokens for OpenAI-compat providers when stream usage is absent

- severity `S2` · confidence `likely` · fix_class `AUTO-REVIEW` · design_impact `none`
- **evidence:** src/lib/providers/openai-compat.ts:20-23 deliberately omits stream_options.include_usage (Mistral 422 lesson) and relies on providers reporting usage 'unprompted in the final chunk' — unverifiable per provider. When none arrives, src/lib/providers/adapter.ts:146-147 estimates tokenOut from raw.length/4, but raw only accumulates POST-filter text: openai-compat.ts:117-119 strips MiniMax <think>...</think> spans before yielding, DashScope reasoning rides delta.reasoning_content which is never read (openai-compat.ts:90-94) with a thinking_budget up to 4096 tokens (QWEN_THINKING_BUDGET, openai-compat.ts:258-264), and DeepSeek/Z.ai reasoning_content is likewise never read (openai-compat.ts:209,281). All of those tokens are billed by the provider but invisible to the estimate, so the daily cost cap (COST_CAP_USD_PER_DAY, config.ts:175) undercounts on exactly the runs that think hardest. Same shape on the abort path: route.ts:252-259 counts streamedChars from decoded output deltas only.
- **blast_radius:** src/lib/providers/adapter.ts, src/lib/providers/openai-compat.ts, src/app/api/enhance/route.ts; ledger accuracy for deepseek_v4, muse_spark_1_1, minimax_m3, kimi_k3, sonar_pro, qwen3_7_max, glm_5_2 targets
- **proposed_fix:** Have the compat stream also count pre-filter chars (and add the active thinking_budget when enable_thinking was sent) into the fallback estimate, e.g. yield a rawChars/estimate hint or accumulate estimate-side chars before the think filter; keep provider-reported usage authoritative when present.
- **verification:** Unit test in tests/unit/compat-adapter.test.ts: stream a response containing a 4000-char <think> span and no usage chunk; assert the adapter's fallback tokenOut reflects the stripped span (currently it does not)
- **disposition:** resolved — Stage 2 W2: compat stream counts stripped think spans + reasoning_content chars and yields estReasoningTokens; adapter adds the floor to its no-usage fallback; compat-stream.test.ts + usage-estimated.test.ts pin it

### PRV-004 — Provider key env names are duplicated between PROVIDER_KEY_ENV and per-adapter hardcoded strings with no test pinning the correspondence

- severity `S3` · confidence `certain` · fix_class `AUTO-SAFE` · design_impact `none`
- **evidence:** src/lib/providers/config.ts:178-191 defines PROVIDER_KEY_ENV (used by route.ts:197 isProviderConfigured pre-stream 503 gate and vision.ts:260 fallback selection); the same names are independently hardcoded in anthropic.ts:56, openai.ts:26, google.ts:41, mistral.ts:25, xai.ts:28 and as keyEnv literals in openai-compat.ts:213,222,230,238,244,273,286. Verified pairwise consistent today (all 12 match). grep of tests/ shows no test imports PROVIDER_KEY_ENV; a future drift would make the pre-stream 503 gate and the adapter's own key check disagree, producing the mid-stream ProviderNotConfiguredError that gate exists to prevent.
- **blast_radius:** src/lib/providers/config.ts plus 7 adapter files; POST /api/enhance 503 contract
- **proposed_fix:** Add a unit test asserting, for every Provider, that the adapter's key env name equals PROVIDER_KEY_ENV[provider] (export the compat opts or read the source constants); no runtime change.
- **verification:** npx vitest run tests/unit/<new>provider-key-env.test.ts passes and fails when any single name is edited
- **disposition:** resolved — Stage 2 W2: provider-policy.test.ts asserts every adapter's key env equals PROVIDER_KEY_ENV per provider

### PRV-005 — Anthropic adapter error copy says 'Opus' for all three Anthropic targets, mislabeling Fable 5 and Sonnet 5 failures in user-facing messages

- severity `S3` · confidence `certain` · fix_class `AUTO-REVIEW` · design_impact `none`
- **evidence:** src/lib/providers/anthropic.ts:85 `Opus request failed: ${error.message}` and :92 'Unknown Opus error.' — this one stream function serves opus_5, sonnet_5 AND fable_5 (config.ts:34-45,68-73); the message reaches the client via the route's SSE error event (route.ts:338-342) and the composer error surface, so a Fable 5 outage reads as an Opus failure.
- **blast_radius:** src/lib/providers/anthropic.ts; enhance error surface for fable_5 and sonnet_5 runs
- **proposed_fix:** Change the label to 'Anthropic request failed:' / 'Unknown Anthropic error.' (matching the provider-named pattern every other adapter uses).
