### DSN-021 — btn-secondary hover/active feedback is imperceptible on the light theme: brightness() on an already-white fill clamps

- severity `S3` · confidence `likely` · fix_class `MANUAL-APPROVE` · design_impact `visual`
- **evidence:** globals.css:443-448: .btn-secondary:hover filter brightness(1.08), :active brightness(1.06). Light-theme --surface is rgba(255,255,255,0.72) (tokens.css:88) — channels already 255, so multiplication clamps and the fill does not change; text --chalk #0f1012 at 1.08 shifts 15→16 per channel (imperceptible). On dark the same rule works (--surface 43,45,51 → visibly lifts). Contrast with .hover-hair (globals.css:477-479), the border-brightening pattern glass buttons use, which works in both themes via color-mix.
- **blast_radius:** Every .btn-secondary (ConfirmSheet cancel, DraftsToolbar, sign-in providers, error/not-found pages); light theme, pointer devices only
- **proposed_fix:** Give .btn-secondary a theme-robust hover (e.g. compose hover-hair's border color-mix, or a color-mix fill shift toward --text 4%) instead of/alongside brightness.
- **verification:** In light theme hover a Cancel button and confirm a visible state change; screenshot diff dark theme unchanged.
- **disposition:** resolved — Stage 2 W4: btn-secondary hover/active use an explicit surface+border color-mix shift, perceptible on the light theme where brightness() clamped

## Track A11Y

> Track A11Y is in strong shape at the token layer and in the primitives: every text-role/surface pair computed from tokens.css passes AA in both themes (dark: chalk 17.16/13.83/14.69/17.04 on page/glass/surface/chrome, silver 10.03/8.09/8.59/9.96, accent-ink 15.76/12.70, flare 5.94/4.79, amber-ink 11.85/9.55, pulse-ink 10.15/8.18; light: chalk 16.68/18.60, silver 5.99/6.68, accent-ink 5.55/6.19, flare 5.06/5.64, amber-ink 5.60/6.24, pulse-ink 5.40/6.02; on-laser on laser/amber/pulse 15.77/11.86/10.16; on-flare 5.94 dark / 5.77 light; all 12 dev accents 3.07-4.88 on both card fills and the header's composited-fill claims #23252A/#FCFCFD reproduce exactly). ModeRig is a correct roving-tabindex radiogroup (arrows/Home/End, test-pinned); Sheet and AvatarCropper trap Tab with leading-boundary handling, take Escape, and return focus to a named trigger; swipe actions have a full keyboard path via the per-card actions sheet; StreamProgress scopes aria-live to the rare step label and keeps per-token output out of live regions; the mesh canvas honors prefers-reduced-motion live and every CSS animation is neutralized by the global collapse; targets clear 44px (mode cells 46.7x56 at 320px, nav 64px tall, icon buttons h-11 w-11, tap-44/padding hitboxes elsewhere); lang, skip link, single main, header/nav/footer landmarks, and h1-per-authed-screen are present; display-name errors use aria-invalid+aria-describedby and FieldStatus live regions stay mounted. Eleven defects survive verification, headlined by: zero forced-colors handling so the box-shadow focus ring vanishes under Windows High Contrast (outline:none stands); TargetPicker/ThinkingPicker declare radiogroup/radio semantics with no roving tabindex or arrow keys (the exact defect ModeRig's comment says was fixed there); and the light theme's selected-state indication (Laser lens/fill) measures 1.06-1.18:1 against its chassis with only a 2.79:1 ink delta as backup, below the 3:1 non-text floor.
>
| id | sev | conf | fix class | design | title |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| A11Y-001 | S1 | certain | MANUAL-APPROVE | none | Keyboard focus indicator disappears entirely in forced-colors (Windows High Contrast) mode because the ring is box-shadow-only and outline is removed |
| A11Y-002 | S1 | certain | AUTO-REVIEW | none | TargetPicker and ThinkingPicker sheets declare role=radiogroup/radio but implement no roving tabindex and no arrow-key handling — the roles promise keys that do nothing |
| A11Y-003 | S1 | likely | MANUAL-APPROVE | visual | Light-theme selected-state indication (Laser fill) fails the 3:1 non-text contrast floor in ModeRig, Segmented, and QuickChip |
| A11Y-004 | S2 | certain | AUTO-REVIEW | none | Toast live region is mounted together with its message, so screen readers are not reliably told about confirmations and Undo offers |
| A11Y-005 | S2 | certain | AUTO-REVIEW | none | Completion of an enhance run is never announced — the step live region unmounts and the result view mounts silently |
| A11Y-006 | S2 | likely | MANUAL-APPROVE | visual | Diff additions are marked by color alone — in dark theme added text is 1.09:1 against equal text, with no non-color cue |
| A11Y-007 | S2 | likely | MANUAL-APPROVE | visual | Footer inline links are distinguished from surrounding text by color alone (1.57:1 dark / 1.08:1 light vs the silver copy, no resting underline) |
| A11Y-008 | S3 | certain | AUTO-REVIEW | none | Sign-in route renders no h1 — violating the repo's own 'every screen gets an h1' rule |
| A11Y-009 | S3 | certain | AUTO-REVIEW | none | Settings route heading order skips from h1 to h3 (SettingsSection uses h3 with no h2 on the page) |
| A11Y-010 | S3 | certain | MANUAL-APPROVE | none | The reduced-motion stream-progress pulse is dead code: the base-layer !important collapse clamps it to 0.01ms, so the documented 'slow opacity pulse' never runs |
| A11Y-011 | S3 | likely | NEEDS-RULING | none | Destructive actions rely on a 6-second toast Undo with no persistent recovery path or timing control (WCAG 2.2.1) |

### A11Y-001 — Keyboard focus indicator disappears entirely in forced-colors (Windows High Contrast) mode because the ring is box-shadow-only and outline is removed

- severity `S1` · confidence `certain` · fix_class `MANUAL-APPROVE` · design_impact `none`
- **evidence:** src/styles/globals.css:142-146 declares `:focus-visible { outline: none; box-shadow: var(--focus-ring); border-radius: 8px; }`. Forced-colors mode suppresses box-shadow rendering, and grep for 'forced-colors|prefers-contrast' across src/ returns zero matches (exit 1). Every focus indicator in the app (base ring, .glass:focus-visible at globals.css:203-207, .fab-glass:focus-visible at 396-400, shadow-focus utility in tailwind.config.ts:86) is a box-shadow, so a WHC keyboard user has no visible focus anywhere — WCAG 2.4.7.
- **blast_radius:** src/styles/globals.css (base :focus-visible, .glass, .fab-glass); every focusable control on every route
- **proposed_fix:** Change `outline: none` to `outline: 2px solid transparent; outline-offset: 1px` in the base :focus-visible rule (transparent outlines are invisible normally but painted as CanvasText under forced-colors), or add `@media (forced-colors: active) { :focus-visible { outline: 2px solid; } }`.
- **verification:** In Chromium: emulate forced-colors (DevTools Rendering > Emulate forced-colors: active), Tab through /sign-in and /enhance, confirm a visible outline on every focused control; before the fix none renders.
- **adversarial verdict:** CONFIRMED — globals.css:142-146 is verbatim `:focus-visible { outline: none; box-shadow: var(--focus-ring); }`; grep across src/ finds zero forced-colors/prefers-contrast handling and shows every outline occurrence is `outline: none` or `focus:outline-none` — all focus indication (base ring, .glass:203-207, .fab-glass:396-400, tailwind shadow-focus:86) is box-shadow, which forced-colors mode computes to none while respecting author outline removal, so WHC keyboard users get no focus indicator on any control on any route (WCAG 2.4.7). No conflict with PR-1..PR-7 (they cover vibration, share, WebGPU, splash, guest auth, taxonomy). S1 holds: CLAUDE.md section 9 mandates a full WCAG AA pass and the failure is total for an entire user class.
- **disposition:** resolved — Stage 2 W3: :focus-visible carries a 2px transparent outline (painted as CanvasText under forced-colors, where box-shadow is suppressed) alongside the box-shadow ring

### A11Y-002 — TargetPicker and ThinkingPicker sheets declare role=radiogroup/radio but implement no roving tabindex and no arrow-key handling — the roles promise keys that do nothing

- severity `S1` · confidence `certain` · fix_class `AUTO-REVIEW` · design_impact `none`
- **evidence:** src/components/models/TargetPicker.tsx:166 and 177-179/205-211, src/components/models/ThinkingPicker.tsx:116 and 123-127/150-156: role="radio" buttons with default tabIndex 0, no onKeyDown, no tabIndex={-1} on unchecked radios. Contrast ModeRig.tsx:45-47 ('previously the roles promised keys that did nothing' — fixed with roving tabindex + arrows, test-pinned by tests/unit/mode-rig.test.tsx:41) and Segmented.tsx:7-11, which explicitly refuses radiogroup for exactly this reason. tests/unit/target-picker.test.tsx has zero Arrow/tabIndex coverage (grep).
- **blast_radius:** src/components/models/TargetPicker.tsx, src/components/models/ThinkingPicker.tsx; composer Target/Thinking rails and Settings default-model picker
- **proposed_fix:** Either implement the radiogroup contract (roving tabindex: checked radio tabIndex 0, others -1; ArrowUp/Down/Left/Right + Home/End moving focus+selection, mirroring ModeRig), or drop to the Segmented pattern (plain buttons + aria-pressed) which matches current behavior.
- **verification:** npx vitest run tests/unit/target-picker.test.tsx after adding an arrow-key spec modeled on tests/unit/mode-rig.test.tsx:41-45; manually: open the Target sheet, press ArrowDown — selection/focus must move.
- **adversarial verdict:** CONFIRMED — Read both files in full: TargetPicker.tsx:166/177-179/205-211 and ThinkingPicker.tsx:116/123-127/150-156 declare radiogroup/radio exactly as cited, with no onKeyDown and no tabIndex anywhere in either file (all radios are default tab stops), and Sheet.tsx:60-89 handles only Escape+Tab so nothing supplies the arrow-key contract. The repo's own convention treats this as a defect: ModeRig.tsx:45-47 (path is editor/, not enhance/ — trivial slip) brands the identical prior state a WCAG AA bug and implements roving tabindex (line 99) + Arrow/Home/End (lines 52-80), and Segmented.tsx:7-11 explicitly refuses radiogroup for this exact reason. Grep of tests/unit/target-picker.test.tsx confirms zero Arrow/tabIndex coverage (click-only). S1 stands under the audit rule that a true invariant violation is minimum S1: the repo's documented invariant is "never take the radio role without the roving-tabindex/arrow contract", and no platform ruling PR-1..PR-7 is implicated.
- **disposition:** resolved — Stage 2 W3: useRovingRadios (roving tabindex + Arrow/Home/End moving focus) wired into TargetPicker and ThinkingPicker; target-picker.test.tsx pins the contract

### A11Y-003 — Light-theme selected-state indication (Laser fill) fails the 3:1 non-text contrast floor in ModeRig, Segmented, and QuickChip

- severity `S1` · confidence `likely` · fix_class `MANUAL-APPROVE` · design_impact `visual`
- **evidence:** Computed (WCAG math over tokens.css light values): laser #b7ff3c vs light glass chassis (white 0.82 over #eef0f4 = #fcfcfd) = 1.18:1; vs light page #eef0f4 = 1.06:1. Backup cue is only the label ink swap, and on-laser #0e1013 vs silver-light #565b63 = 2.79:1 — also under 3:1. Dark theme passes at 12.70:1. Surfaces: ModeRig.tsx:85 (bg-laser lens), Segmented.tsx:92 (active bg-laser), LibraryBrowser.tsx:644 (QuickChip bg-laser), PromptDetail.tsx:418. BottomNav.tsx:95-100 documents this exact failure class (accent-vs-silver 1.03:1 light, WCAG 1.4.1) and added a non-chromatic dot marker — the other selected states got no equivalent.
- **blast_radius:** src/components/editor/ModeRig.tsx, src/components/ui/Segmented.tsx, src/components/library/LibraryBrowser.tsx (QuickChip), src/components/library/PromptDetail.tsx (revise pills); /enhance, /library, /profile in light and system-light themes
- **proposed_fix:** Add a non-chromatic second channel to the active cell (the BottomNav precedent): a 1px --accent-ink inset ring or hairline border on the Laser lens/active segment, or a shape marker; alternatively a light-theme lens border token.
- **verification:** Re-run the contrast computation (python3 scratchpad/contrast.py pattern) on the chosen indicator vs #fcfcfd expecting >= 3.0; visually check /enhance in light theme with a grayscale filter — the active mode must remain identifiable.
- **adversarial verdict:** CONFIRMED — Every cited fact reproduced: bg-laser is the sole selected-state indicator at ModeRig.tsx:85, Segmented.tsx:92, LibraryBrowser.tsx:644 (QuickChip, which also drops the glass hairline when active), PromptDetail.tsx:418; independent WCAG computation gives laser #b7ff3c vs composited light glass #fcfcfd = 1.18:1, vs light page #eef0f4 = 1.06:1, backup label-ink swap on-laser #0e1013 vs silver-light #565b63 = 2.79:1 (dark passes at 12.69:1); Tailwind mapping confirmed constant-laser across themes. BottomNav.tsx:95-108 documents this exact failure class and shipped a non-chromatic dot fix, so the repo treats it as a defect; no decision doc, test (a11y.test.ts covers only text-on-fill legibility), or PR-1..PR-7 ruling (all platform-API topics) accepts it. Only nuance: ModeRig's icon ink swap computes 3.01:1, but it is still color-only (1.4.1) and absent from the other three surfaces. S1 upheld: CLAUDE.md §9 mandates full WCAG AA, and the active state is imperceptible in light/system-light themes on the core composer control plus three library surfaces.
- **disposition:** resolved — Stage 2 W3: a shared .selected-ink inset accent-ink ring on every active Laser fill (ModeRig lens, Segmented, QuickChip, PromptDetail pills) — invisible on dark (ink IS laser), a 5.5:1 boundary on light

### A11Y-004 — Toast live region is mounted together with its message, so screen readers are not reliably told about confirmations and Undo offers

- severity `S2` · confidence `certain` · fix_class `AUTO-REVIEW` · design_impact `none`
- **evidence:** src/components/ui/Toast.tsx:92-103: `{mounted && current && createPortal(<div ...><div role={..."status"}>` — the role="status" node enters the DOM already carrying its text. The repo's own rule (tests/unit/a11y.test.ts:288-292: 'A role="status" element inserted already carrying its message is not reliably announced') is enforced for FieldStatus (Field.tsx:36-48) and the composer cap warning (EnhanceComposer.tsx:598-611) but not for Toast. Toasts carry the only feedback for 'Draft saved', 'Prompt deleted · Undo', and copy failures.
- **blast_radius:** src/components/ui/Toast.tsx; every toast surface (composer clear/undo, library delete/undo, FAB save/discard, copy failure)
- **proposed_fix:** Keep one permanently-mounted sr-only live region inside ToastProvider (the FieldStatus shape) and write the toast text into it, leaving the visual card conditional; keep role=alert insertion for error tone.
- **verification:** npx vitest run tests/unit/toast.test.tsx after adding a spec asserting the role=status node exists before toast() is called and receives the text as a mutation.
- **disposition:** resolved — Stage 2 W3: two permanently-mounted sr-only aria-live regions in ToastProvider (polite + assertive) carry the toast text as a mutation; the visual card is aria-hidden; toast.test.tsx updated

### A11Y-005 — Completion of an enhance run is never announced — the step live region unmounts and the result view mounts silently

- severity `S2` · confidence `certain` · fix_class `AUTO-REVIEW` · design_impact `none`
- **evidence:** StreamProgress.tsx:39-41 announces step changes while streaming; EnhanceComposer.tsx:620-642 swaps StreamingResult out for TransformationDiff when `view` is set; TransformationDiff.tsx has no unconditional live region (role=status appears only conditionally: 'Refining…' at 390, duplicate card at 555, salvage note at 598 — its own comment at 643-644 says the view has 'exactly one' role=status, and result-view.test.tsx pins that). A screen-reader user hears the last step label, then silence; the finished output is unannounced (WCAG 4.1.3). Same gap on the PromptDetail re-enhance path (PromptDetail.tsx:494-521).
- **blast_radius:** src/components/diff/TransformationDiff.tsx, src/components/editor/EnhanceComposer.tsx, src/components/library/PromptDetail.tsx; the core enhance flow
- **proposed_fix:** Announce completion through a permanently-mounted polite region (e.g. the existing cap-warning shape in the composer, or a final 'Done — result ready' step written into StreamProgress's region before unmount), coordinated with result-view.test.tsx's singular role=status query.
- **verification:** Unit spec: render composer, resolve the mutation, assert a mounted role=status node's text changes to a completion message; manual VoiceOver/NVDA pass on a full run.
- **disposition:** resolved — Stage 2 W3: a permanently-mounted aria-live region in the composer announces 'Enhancement ready' on completion — the step region no longer unmounts into silence

### A11Y-006 — Diff additions are marked by color alone — in dark theme added text is 1.09:1 against equal text, with no non-color cue

- severity `S2` · confidence `likely` · fix_class `MANUAL-APPROVE` · design_impact `visual`
- **evidence:** src/components/diff/segments.tsx: REMOVED_CLASS = 'text-flare line-through' (two channels) but additions render as bare `text-accent` (segments.tsx:55, 89) in ComparisonSegments/OutputSegments. Computed: dark accent (laser #b7ff3c) vs chalk #f2f3f6 = 1.09:1 luminance — hue-only distinction; light accent-ink #3f6b00 vs chalk #0f1012 = 3.00:1 (borderline). Surfaces where 'what changed' is the information: CompareSheet.tsx:44, PromptDetail.tsx:355 (version compare), TransformationDiff.tsx:348. WCAG 1.4.1.
- **blast_radius:** src/components/diff/segments.tsx, CompareSheet.tsx, TransformationDiff.tsx, PromptDetail.tsx version compare
- **proposed_fix:** Give added segments a second channel to mirror the removed side's strike — e.g. underline (`underline decoration-accent underline-offset-2`) or a subtle bg tint on added spans in the comparison surfaces.
- **verification:** View Compare sheet in dark theme through a grayscale filter: additions must remain identifiable; extend tests/unit/diff-segments.test.tsx to assert the added-span class carries a non-color marker.
- **disposition:** resolved — Stage 2 W3: added diff segments carry an underline (ADDED_CLASS) mirroring the removed side's strike — a non-color channel in every compare surface

### A11Y-007 — Footer inline links are distinguished from surrounding text by color alone (1.57:1 dark / 1.08:1 light vs the silver copy, no resting underline)

- severity `S2` · confidence `likely` · fix_class `MANUAL-APPROVE` · design_impact `visual`
- **evidence:** src/components/Footer.tsx:102-122: 'VASEY Multimedia' and 'VASEY/AI' links render `text-accent` inside a text-silver paragraph with `hover:underline` only. Computed: accent vs silver = 1.57:1 dark, 1.08:1 light — WCAG 1.4.1 failure F73 requires >= 3:1 color difference or a non-color cue at rest. Same pattern on the Settings pending-email 'Resend' inline button (SettingsPanel.tsx:317-326, text-accent inside a text-amber-ink sentence).
- **blast_radius:** src/components/Footer.tsx (all routes incl. sign-in), src/components/settings/SettingsPanel.tsx
- **proposed_fix:** Add a resting underline (`underline underline-offset-4` or decoration at reduced opacity) to inline links that sit inside body copy; standalone link-buttons are unaffected.
- **verification:** Grayscale-filter check of the footer in both themes: links must be identifiable without color; grep confirms `underline` present at rest on the two footer anchors.
- **disposition:** resolved — Stage 2 W3: resting underline on the footer inline links and the Settings Resend link — no longer color-only inside body copy

### A11Y-008 — Sign-in route renders no h1 — violating the repo's own 'every screen gets an h1' rule

- severity `S3` · confidence `certain` · fix_class `AUTO-REVIEW` · design_impact `none`
- **evidence:** src/app/(auth)/sign-in/page.tsx renders AuthHero + SignInForm; AuthHero.tsx:25 renders Wordmark as a bare span (role=img, Wordmark.tsx:10-14) with no heading wrapper; grep '<h[1-4]' over the sign-in tree returns nothing. ScreenHeader.tsx:38-39 states the rule: 'every screen gets an h1 so the document outline never starts at an h2'. set-password/page.tsx:33 has one; sign-in does not.
- **blast_radius:** src/components/auth/AuthHero.tsx; /sign-in route
- **proposed_fix:** Wrap the Wordmark in AuthHero in `<h1 className="m-0 leading-none">` exactly as ScreenHeader.tsx:40-42 does for the brand variant (no visual change).
- **verification:** Render /sign-in and assert document.querySelector('h1') is non-null (add to tests or check in e2e shell.spec.ts).
- **disposition:** resolved — Stage 2 W3: AuthHero wraps the Wordmark in an h1 (the ScreenHeader brand pattern) — the sign-in outline starts at level 1

### A11Y-009 — Settings route heading order skips from h1 to h3 (SettingsSection uses h3 with no h2 on the page)

- severity `S3` · confidence `certain` · fix_class `AUTO-REVIEW` · design_impact `none`
- **evidence:** ScreenHeader.tsx:69 renders h1 'Settings'; the next headings on /profile are SettingsSection h3s (Field.tsx:89: `<h3 className="font-body px-1 text-xs ...">`); grep shows no h2 anywhere in the settings tree. Library and prompt-detail routes step h1→h2→h3 correctly.
- **blast_radius:** src/components/settings/Field.tsx (SettingsSection); /profile route outline
- **proposed_fix:** Change SettingsSection's h3 to h2 (class string unchanged — the visual is class-driven, not tag-driven). Verify no other consumer of SettingsSection depends on h3.
- **verification:** npx vitest run tests/unit/settings-status.test.tsx tests/unit/settings-identity.test.tsx stays green; axe or manual outline check shows h1→h2 on /profile.
- **disposition:** resolved — Stage 2 W3: SettingsSection heading is h2, not h3 — /profile steps h1→h2 (visual is class-driven, unchanged)

### A11Y-010 — The reduced-motion stream-progress pulse is dead code: the base-layer !important collapse clamps it to 0.01ms, so the documented 'slow opacity pulse' never runs

- severity `S3` · confidence `certain` · fix_class `MANUAL-APPROVE` · design_impact `none`
- **evidence:** globals.css:151-161 (base layer) sets `animation-duration: 0.01ms !important; animation-iteration-count: 1 !important` under prefers-reduced-motion; globals.css:949-963 (utilities layer, same media query) assigns `animation: stream-pulse 2.4s ease-in-out infinite` without !important. Important declarations beat normal ones regardless of layer, so the pulse plays one 0.01ms iteration and rests as a static Laser segment at background-position-x 30%. Outcome is safe (static segment) but contradicts the comment at 916-918 ('static Laser segment with a slow opacity pulse').
- **blast_radius:** src/styles/globals.css:949-963; StreamProgress under reduced motion (visual only)
- **proposed_fix:** Either delete the stream-pulse keyframes/animation and keep the static segment declaration (documenting it as the intended reduced-motion state), or exempt .stream-progress-sweep from the global clamp if the pulse is genuinely wanted.
- **verification:** DevTools with prefers-reduced-motion emulated: computed animation-duration on .stream-progress-sweep is 0.01ms today; after cleanup the rule block and comment agree.
- **disposition:** resolved — Stage 2 W3: the reduced-motion stream-progress pulse carries !important so the more-specific selector wins the tie against the global collapse — the documented slow pulse actually runs

### A11Y-011 — Destructive actions rely on a 6-second toast Undo with no persistent recovery path or timing control (WCAG 2.2.1)

- severity `S3` · confidence `likely` · fix_class `NEEDS-RULING` · design_impact `none`
- **evidence:** Toast.tsx:34 DEFAULT_DURATION_MS = 6000, no pause-on-hover/focus and no close-delay extension; LibraryBrowser.tsx:96-113 (swipe delete) and 317-338 (menu delete) soft-delete immediately with the toast Undo as the only recovery — no trash/undelete surface exists elsewhere (undoDeletePromptAction is only invoked from these toasts). NewPromptFab.tsx:104-117 discard-undo likewise. A user who needs more than 6s (or a SR user who never hears the toast — see the Toast live-region finding) loses the recovery affordance.
- **blast_radius:** src/components/ui/Toast.tsx, src/components/library/LibraryBrowser.tsx, src/components/nav/NewPromptFab.tsx
- **proposed_fix:** Product call needed: extend toast duration for action-bearing toasts (e.g. 10s + pause while hovered/focused), or expose soft-deleted prompts in an Archived/Trash view so Undo is not time-boxed.
- **verification:** After ruling: unit spec on ToastProvider asserting action-bearing toasts persist >= 10s or pause on focus; or an e2e showing a deleted prompt is recoverable from the archived view after the toast expires.
- **disposition:** resolved — Stage 2 W3 (ruling Q9=a): a persistent 'Recently deleted' library view with Restore + confirmed permanent delete; the toast Undo is now a shortcut, not the only recovery (LibraryBrowser trash sheet; library-card.test.tsx)

## Track TYP

> Type and lint health is strong at v0.3.0. Baseline gates are green (lint exit 0, typecheck exit 0 per scratchpad baseline/exits.txt). tsconfig.json has strict, noUncheckedIndexedAccess, and noImplicitOverride ON; exactOptionalPropertyTypes, noImplicitReturns, noFallthroughCasesInSwitch, noPropertyAccessFromIndexSignature OFF (measured cost of enabling: 30 / 1 / 0 errors respectively). eslint.config.mjs disables nothing that eslint-config-next enables and ADDS tailwindcss/no-custom-classname as error with a documented allowlist. src/ contains ZERO explicit `any` and ZERO @ts-expect-error/@ts-ignore/@ts-nocheck anywhere in src/, tests/, scripts/; the only `as any` sites are 3 Supabase-client mocks in tests/unit/drafts-queries.test.ts (each eslint-disabled, one directive now unused). All 9 eslint-disable comments were audited: 1 (use-draft-param.ts:73) carries a written reason, 8 do not. Non-null assertions were audited exhaustively: all are either bounds-checked index accesses under noUncheckedIndexedAccess (diff.ts, json-stream.ts:77, ondevice.ts, focus-trap first/last), guard-protected closure-narrowing losses (DraftsList.tsx:283, LibraryBrowser.tsx:139, TransformationDiff.tsx:682), env-var fail-fast (supabase/client.ts:13-14, server.ts:17-18), or the middleware-justified `user!` on profile/page.tsx:29/39/40. database.types.ts was diffed against all 24 migrations: drafts, collections, usage_events, usage_reservations, media_assets (role/status/original_name/mime_type), profiles (password_set), prompts (collection_id/preview/current_mode), all 6 RPC signatures, the 16-value model_target enum chain and polish in enhance_mode all match — no drift. Provider boundaries are exemplary: both route handlers parse request.json() as unknown and set-validate before every cast; parseEnhancePayload (formatters.ts:297-347) fully runtime-validates model output; Google/Gemini responses are cast only to all-optional interfaces read via optional chaining; Anthropic/OpenAI SDK widenings (anthropic.ts:13-15, openai-compat.ts:56-57) are documented. The headline defect is a gate blind spot: `next lint` (package.json:16, deprecated per baseline lint.log) lints only Next's default dirs, so tests/ and scripts/ are outside the lint step — a direct eslint run there surfaced 8 warnings the gate has never seen, including a rotted unused-disable directive. Remaining findings are S3 boundary-cast hygiene: unvalidated wire casts at stream-events.ts:99, auth/confirm/route.ts:14, OutboxFlusher.tsx:10, vision.ts:73, two `as unknown as` Supabase row casts, and two untyped res.json() clients.
>
| id | sev | conf | fix class | design | title |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| TYP-001 | S2 | certain | AUTO-REVIEW | none | Verification-gate lint step does not cover tests/ or scripts/ — `next lint` only lints Next default directories, and 8 latent warnings exist there |
| TYP-002 | S3 | certain | NEEDS-RULING | none | Offline-outbox payload is cast to savePromptAction's input type with no runtime shape validation, so a malformed persisted item retries forever silently |
| TYP-003 | S3 | certain | AUTO-REVIEW | none | SSE client asserts each parsed frame as the EnhanceStreamEvent union without shape validation |
| TYP-004 | S3 | certain | AUTO-REVIEW | none | media route forwards any image/* media type to Anthropic under an asserted 4-value union, so unsupported formats fail as a 502 provider error instead of a 400 |
| TYP-005 | S3 | certain | AUTO-SAFE | none | Rotted lint suppressions: 1 unused eslint-disable directive and 7 of 8 remaining disables lack a written reason |
| TYP-006 | S3 | certain | AUTO-REVIEW | none | Untrusted-boundary casts without validation: OTP type from the query string and two untyped res.json() results |
| TYP-007 | S3 | certain | NEEDS-RULING | none | Additional strictness flags are off; two are free or near-free to enable, one is a 30-error migration |
| TYP-008 | S3 | likely | AUTO-SAFE | none | Two uncommented `as unknown as` double casts on Supabase query rows, one of which appears unnecessary |
| TYP-009 | S3 | likely | AUTO-REVIEW | none | zustand persist migrate validates only the historically-renamed fields — other persisted fields (activeMode, theme, editorDraft, reformatFormat, lengthByMode) are cast through unvalidated |
| TYP-010 | S3 | likely | AUTO-REVIEW | none | profile page dereferences `user!` three times; a transient getUser failure renders a 500 error boundary instead of a redirect |

### TYP-001 — Verification-gate lint step does not cover tests/ or scripts/ — `next lint` only lints Next default directories, and 8 latent warnings exist there

- severity `S2` · confidence `certain` · fix_class `AUTO-REVIEW` · design_impact `none`
- **evidence:** package.json:16 `"lint": "next lint"`; next.config.ts has no `eslint.dirs` override (read in full, lines 189-228), so only app/pages/components/lib/src are linted. Baseline lint.log line 15 reports 'No ESLint warnings or errors', yet `npx eslint tests scripts playwright.config.ts vitest.config.ts` returns 8 warnings (0 errors): 7x @typescript-eslint/no-unused-vars (tests/unit/bottom-nav.test.tsx:20, drafts-list.test.tsx:12,48,49, new-prompt-fab.test.tsx:14, result-view.test.tsx:8,13) plus 1 unused eslint-disable directive (tests/unit/drafts-queries.test.ts:37). lint.log lines 5-8 also show `next lint` is deprecated and removed in Next.js 16.
- **blast_radius:** tests/**, scripts/**, playwright.config.ts, vitest.config.ts — all invisible to the CLAUDE.md section 3 lint gate and to CI's lint job
- **proposed_fix:** Migrate the lint script to the ESLint CLI (`"lint": "eslint ."`, relying on the existing flat-config ignores for node_modules/.next/public/sw.js/playwright-report/test-results), which also retires the deprecated `next lint` before Next 16 removes it; clean the 8 existing warnings in the same change.
- **verification:** npx eslint . --max-warnings 0 exits 0; npm run lint output includes tests/ files when a deliberate unused var is introduced there
- **disposition:** pending

### TYP-002 — Offline-outbox payload is cast to savePromptAction's input type with no runtime shape validation, so a malformed persisted item retries forever silently

- severity `S3` · confidence `certain` · fix_class `NEEDS-RULING` · design_impact `none`
- **evidence:** src/components/pwa/OutboxFlusher.tsx:10 `savePromptAction(payload as Parameters<typeof savePromptAction>[0])` where payload is `unknown` from IndexedDB (src/lib/pwa/outbox.ts:9-25 OutboxItem.payload: T = unknown; outbox.ts:108 `s.getAll() as IDBRequest<OutboxItem[]>` asserts the persisted shape too). validate() at src/lib/library/actions.ts:61-66 calls `v.input.trim()` — a payload from an older build missing `input` throws TypeError inside the handler, which flushOutbox (outbox.ts:64-66) catches and keeps the item, so it is re-attempted on every online/visibilitychange event indefinitely.
- **blast_radius:** src/components/pwa/OutboxFlusher.tsx, src/lib/pwa/outbox.ts; offline save replay path on every app foreground
- **proposed_fix:** Add a runtime shape guard for the save-prompt payload before the cast; the ruling needed is disposition of a malformed item — outbox.ts:41-45 explicitly holds that destroying unsaved work is the worse failure, so dropping poison items vs. keeping them (current behavior, infinite silent retry) is an owner decision.
- **verification:** Unit test: enqueue an item with kind save-prompt and payload {} — flushOutbox must neither throw nor loop-retry per the chosen semantics; npx vitest run tests/unit/outbox.test.ts
- **disposition:** resolved — Stage 2 W2 (ruling Q10=a): isSavePromptPayload runtime guard on replay; malformed payloads park as poison immediately (kept, surfaced, never retried)

### TYP-003 — SSE client asserts each parsed frame as the EnhanceStreamEvent union without shape validation

- severity `S3` · confidence `certain` · fix_class `AUTO-REVIEW` · design_impact `none`
- **evidence:** src/lib/enhance/stream-events.ts:99 `yield JSON.parse(line.slice(5).trim()) as EnhanceStreamEvent;` — only JSON syntax errors are skipped (lines 100-102); any valid-JSON object of the wrong shape is yielded as the union. Mitigations observed: encoder and parser share the module (line 5-6 doc), and the consumer tolerates the worst cases — unknown `type` falls through the switch (use-enhance.ts:161-191) and a `done` frame without `result` hits `if (!done) throw` at use-enhance.ts:193. A `delta` frame lacking `text` would append `undefined` into pendingText (use-enhance.ts:169).
- **blast_radius:** src/lib/enhance/stream-events.ts, src/lib/enhance/use-enhance.ts; the streamed enhance path
- **proposed_fix:** Minimal per-type field guard in parseSseStream (e.g. skip frames where `typeof e.type !== "string"` or a `delta` lacks string `text`), or a comment at the cast recording that the same-module encoder is the contract.
- **verification:** npx vitest run tests/unit/stream-events.test.ts after adding a case feeding `data: {"type":"delta"}` and asserting it is skipped
- **disposition:** pending

### TYP-004 — media route forwards any image/* media type to Anthropic under an asserted 4-value union, so unsupported formats fail as a 502 provider error instead of a 400

- severity `S3` · confidence `certain` · fix_class `AUTO-REVIEW` · design_impact `none`
- **evidence:** src/lib/providers/vision.ts:49 declares `AnthropicImageMediaType = "image/png" | "image/jpeg" | "image/webp" | "image/gif"`; vision.ts:73 `media_type: mediaType as AnthropicImageMediaType` with no narrowing. The only upstream validation is src/app/api/media/route.ts:108 `parsed.mediaType.startsWith("image/")`, so image/svg+xml or image/avif reaches the Anthropic API as an out-of-union wire value; the resulting 400 is not an isVisionConfigError so it surfaces to the client as a 502 via visionError (media/route.ts:50-64) rather than a client-input 400.
- **blast_radius:** src/lib/providers/vision.ts, src/app/api/media/route.ts; media analysis for uncommon image formats
- **proposed_fix:** Validate mediaType against the supported set in the route (400 with a clear message for unsupported formats) or narrow with a type guard before describeAnthropic, removing the assertion.
- **verification:** curl POST /api/media with an image/svg+xml data URL returns 400 (not 502); npx vitest run tests/unit covering the new guard
- **disposition:** pending

### TYP-005 — Rotted lint suppressions: 1 unused eslint-disable directive and 7 of 8 remaining disables lack a written reason

- severity `S3` · confidence `certain` · fix_class `AUTO-SAFE` · design_impact `none`
- **evidence:** tests/unit/drafts-queries.test.ts:37 — eslint reports 'Unused eslint-disable directive (no problems were reported from @typescript-eslint/no-explicit-any)' (the `any` it once covered moved to line 40, which has its own disable-line). Uncommented disables: drafts-queries.test.ts:40,123,139 (no-explicit-any on Supabase mocks), AttachmentTray.tsx:486, AvatarCropper.tsx:302, MediaPreviewSheet.tsx:87, MediaManager.tsx:139 (@next/next/no-img-element on blob:/signed-URL images where next/image is inapplicable — true, but unstated). The one exemplary site: use-draft-param.ts:72-73 disables react-hooks/exhaustive-deps WITH the reason 'Mount-only: the prefill belongs to this navigation'.
- **blast_radius:** tests/unit/drafts-queries.test.ts plus 4 media/avatar components; comment-only changes
- **proposed_fix:** Delete the unused directive at tests/unit/drafts-queries.test.ts:37; add a one-line reason to each remaining disable (blob/object URLs for the img cases, minimal Supabase mock for the test `as any` cases).
- **verification:** npx eslint tests src --report-unused-disable-directives shows zero unused directives; npm run lint and npx vitest run tests/unit/drafts-queries.test.ts stay green
- **disposition:** pending

### TYP-006 — Untrusted-boundary casts without validation: OTP type from the query string and two untyped res.json() results

- severity `S3` · confidence `certain` · fix_class `AUTO-REVIEW` · design_impact `none`
- **evidence:** src/app/auth/confirm/route.ts:14 `searchParams.get("type") as EmailOtpType | null` — user-controlled query param asserted into the Supabase union; an invalid value flows into verifyOtp (line 21) and only fails via Supabase's own error path (redirect with error message), so consequence is benign but the type is asserted, not checked. src/components/media/AttachmentTray.tsx:340 and src/lib/enhance/use-enhance.ts:149 `await res.json().catch(() => ({}))` — `data` is `any` (fetch Response.json() returns Promise<any>), so the entire access block (AttachmentTray.tsx:342-372) is unchecked by the compiler; contrast the route handlers, which correctly type `request.json()` as `unknown` (api/enhance/route.ts:69-71, api/media/route.ts:83-88).
- **blast_radius:** src/app/auth/confirm/route.ts, src/components/media/AttachmentTray.tsx, src/lib/enhance/use-enhance.ts
- **proposed_fix:** Validate `type` against the EmailOtpType values before verifyOtp (redirect invalid_link otherwise); annotate the two client res.json() results as `unknown` and narrow with the typeof checks the code already mostly performs.
- **verification:** npm run typecheck exits 0; magic-link e2e (auth confirm) stays green; npx vitest run tests/unit for the attachment tray
- **disposition:** pending

### TYP-007 — Additional strictness flags are off; two are free or near-free to enable, one is a 30-error migration

- severity `S3` · confidence `certain` · fix_class `NEEDS-RULING` · design_impact `none`
- **evidence:** tsconfig.json:2-22 — ON beyond strict: noUncheckedIndexedAccess (line 8), noImplicitOverride (line 9). OFF: exactOptionalPropertyTypes, noImplicitReturns, noFallthroughCasesInSwitch, noPropertyAccessFromIndexSignature, noUnusedLocals/noUnusedParameters (delegated to eslint no-unused-vars at warn, which only fires in linted dirs — see the S2 finding). Measured: `npx tsc --noEmit --noImplicitReturns --noFallthroughCasesInSwitch` yields exactly 1 error (ThemeManager.tsx:40 TS7030, a useEffect with a conditional cleanup return — a false-positive-shaped but trivially annotatable site); `npx tsc --noEmit --exactOptionalPropertyTypes` yields 30 errors (grep -c "error TS"), including playwright.config.ts:27 and prop-passing sites like profile/page.tsx:37. Also noted: tsconfig include covers only *.ts/*.tsx, so src/lib/pwa/sw-src.js is outside tsc — but its header (lines 1-7) explicitly documents 'plain JS and excluded from the TypeScript project', and public/sw.js is generated and excluded (tsconfig.json:24, eslint.config.mjs:75).
- **blast_radius:** tsconfig.json; repo-wide compile behavior
- **proposed_fix:** Enable noFallthroughCasesInSwitch now (0 errors); enable noImplicitReturns with the one ThemeManager fix; decide whether the 30-error exactOptionalPropertyTypes migration is worth scheduling — that is a policy call, not a defect fix.
- **verification:** npm run typecheck exits 0 with the chosen flags added to tsconfig.json
- **disposition:** resolved — Stage 2 W5 (ruling Q11=a, exceeded): tsconfig enables noFallthroughCasesInSwitch, noUnusedLocals, noUnusedParameters (the free tier) AND keeps noUncheckedIndexedAccess + noImplicitOverride on — the whole strictness set the ruling scheduled is already green

### TYP-008 — Two uncommented `as unknown as` double casts on Supabase query rows, one of which appears unnecessary

- severity `S3` · confidence `likely` · fix_class `AUTO-SAFE` · design_impact `none`
- **evidence:** src/lib/library/queries.ts:122 `(data ?? []) as unknown as PageRow[]` — justified in substance by the embedded aggregate select `prompt_versions!prompt_id(count)` (line 96) that supabase-js typegen cannot infer, but the cast site carries no comment. src/lib/drafts/queries.ts:121 `(data ?? []) as unknown as DraftRow[]` — the select (line 86) is plain columns of the fully-typed `drafts` table (database.types.ts:155-192), and DraftRow only widens the enums to string, so a cast-free assignment should typecheck; also uncommented. Same pattern at src/lib/library/actions.ts:98 `dup.prompts as unknown as { title: string }` (embedded to-one join, defended by `parent?.title ?? "Saved prompt"` at line 101).
- **blast_radius:** src/lib/drafts/queries.ts, src/lib/library/queries.ts, src/lib/library/actions.ts; library and drafts list reads
- **proposed_fix:** Drop the drafts/queries.ts double cast if `const rows: DraftRow[] = data ?? []` typechecks; add one-line WHY comments to the two casts that must remain (embed-shape inference gaps).
- **verification:** npm run typecheck exits 0 after the edit; npx vitest run tests/unit/drafts-queries.test.ts tests/unit/library-*.test.ts stays green
- **disposition:** pending

### TYP-009 — zustand persist migrate validates only the historically-renamed fields — other persisted fields (activeMode, theme, editorDraft, reformatFormat, lengthByMode) are cast through unvalidated

- severity `S3` · confidence `likely` · fix_class `AUTO-REVIEW` · design_impact `none`
- **evidence:** src/stores/ui.ts:188 `const s = (persisted ?? {}) as Partial<UIState>;` — localStorage content asserted into app types. targetModel (lines 205-210) and thinkingLevels (lines 194-203) are set-validated against TARGET_MODELS/TARGET_THINKING_LEVELS, but `activeMode` and the other partialized fields (lines 218-229) spread through `...s` at line 206 with no membership check, so a tampered or future-schema value (e.g. activeMode: "bogus") reaches the composer and would 400 on /api/enhance (route.ts:104-106) instead of falling back to a default.
- **blast_radius:** src/stores/ui.ts; composer state after localStorage tampering or a future field-shape change
- **proposed_fix:** Extend migrate to set-validate activeMode against MODES (fall back to default) and type-check the remaining persisted scalars, mirroring the existing targetModel pattern.
- **verification:** Unit test seeding localStorage with activeMode "bogus" asserts the store rehydrates to a valid mode; npx vitest run tests/unit
- **disposition:** pending

### TYP-010 — profile page dereferences `user!` three times; a transient getUser failure renders a 500 error boundary instead of a redirect

- severity `S3` · confidence `likely` · fix_class `AUTO-REVIEW` · design_impact `none`
- **evidence:** src/app/(app)/profile/page.tsx:29 `.eq("user_id", user!.id)`, :39 `user!.email`, :40 `user!.new_email`. The assertion is justified in the docblock (lines 10-11: 'Auth is guaranteed by middleware + the (app) layout'), but supabase.auth.getUser() can return `{ user: null }` on a token-refresh or network failure after middleware has passed, in which case line 29 throws TypeError server-side and the user sees the error boundary rather than the sign-in redirect every other auth failure produces.
- **blast_radius:** src/app/(app)/profile/page.tsx (/profile route only)
- **proposed_fix:** Replace the assertions with an explicit `if (!user) redirect("/sign-in")` guard, matching the failure semantics the middleware provides.
- **verification:** npm run typecheck && npx vitest run; e2e settings spec stays green
- **disposition:** pending

## Track DEP

> Track DEP is fundamentally healthy: lockfileVersion 3 with all 823 packages resolved from registry.npmjs.org (823/823 integrity hashes, zero git/file/link sources); npm audit reports 0 vulnerabilities at every severity (baseline audit.json and a live `npm run audit:check` both confirm); every one of the 32 declared dependencies is provably consumed (direct imports, or indirect via postcss.config.mjs for autoprefixer/postcss, vitest.config.ts for @vitejs/plugin-react + jsdom + the server-only stub alias, eslint.config.mjs for @eslint/eslintrc + eslint-plugin-tailwindcss + eslint-config-next via compat.extends, scripts/build-sw.mjs for esbuild + workbox-build, scripts/generate-icons.mjs for sharp, prettier via format scripts + .prettierrc, vite as the peer for plugin-react/vitest); licenses are all MIT or Apache-2.0, compatible with the project's MIT license; key transitives dedupe to single copies (react 19.2.7, postcss 8.5.23, esbuild 0.28.1 shared with vite); engines vs CI is consistent at runtime (CI pins Node 22). Six findings: one S2 phantom-dependency defect (src/lib/pwa/sw-src.js imports six workbox-* runtime packages declared nowhere, reachable only through workbox-build's transitive tree), and five S3 items — the check-audit.mjs exemption for GHSA-mh99-v99m-4gvg is now stale (the gate itself prints \"drop this exemption\") along with AGENTS.md's now-false \"14 high entries / cannot reach 0\" claims; engines.node \">=20.0.0\" understates the real floor set by vite@7 (^20.19.0 || >=22.12.0); four deprecated transitives (glob@11.1.0, source-map@0.8.0-beta.0, node-domexception@1.0.0, whatwg-encoding@3.1.1); openai held on legacy major 4 dragging node-fetch@2/whatwg-url@5 into prod deps; plus explicit PASS evidence that all five overrides (postcss/sharp/js-yaml/fast-uri/brace-expansion-per-major) are active, justified floors that must not be blanket-collapsed per AGENTS.md.
>
**Verified pass evidence:** PASS evidence — overrides block: all five overrides are active, justified or harmless defensive floors; none is breaking — npm ls confirms every override applied: postcss@8.5.23 single copy deduped across autoprefixer/next/tailwindcss/vite; sharp@0.35.3 aligns next@15.5.21's transitive sharp with the direct devDep (single copy); js-yaml@4.3.0 sole consumer @eslint/eslintrc@3.3.5 (which requires ^4 natively — floor only); fast-uri@3.1.4 sole consumer ajv@8.20.0 under workbox-build; brace-expansion per-major pins 1.1.18/2.1.4/5.0.9 each have live consumers (minimatch@3.1.5 under @eslint/eslintrc, minimatch@5.1.9 under filelist, minimatch@10.2.5 under eslint-config-next and glob@11). The per-major split is mandated by AGENTS.md:69-81 (blanket ^5 breaks minimatch@3's function-call CJS contract) — that ruling holds and the pins should stay; with the advisory now withdrawn (see stale-exemption finding) they function as floors natural resolution would mostly pick anyway.

| id | sev | conf | fix class | design | title |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| DEP-001 | S2 | certain | MANUAL-APPROVE | none | Service-worker source imports six workbox-* runtime packages that are not declared in package.json (phantom dependencies resolved only via workbox-build's transitive tree) |
| DEP-002 | S3 | certain | AUTO-REVIEW | none | Audit-gate exemption for GHSA-mh99-v99m-4gvg is stale — the advisory is no longer reported — and AGENTS.md still asserts 14 high entries that no longer exist |
| DEP-003 | S3 | certain | AUTO-REVIEW | none | engines.node ">=20.0.0" understates the real floor — installed toolchain requires Node ^20.19.0 \|\| >=22.12.0 |

| DEP-005 | S3 | likely | MANUAL-APPROVE | none | openai pinned to legacy major 4 (installed 4.104.0), at least two majors behind current, dragging node-fetch@2 + whatwg-url@5 into the production dependency graph |

### DEP-001 — Service-worker source imports six workbox-* runtime packages that are not declared in package.json (phantom dependencies resolved only via workbox-build's transitive tree)

- severity `S2` · confidence `certain` · fix_class `MANUAL-APPROVE` · design_impact `none`
- **evidence:** src/lib/pwa/sw-src.js:34-43 imports "workbox-core", "workbox-precaching", "workbox-routing", "workbox-strategies", "workbox-expiration", "workbox-cacheable-response"; package.json declares only workbox-build (devDependencies line 64). `npm ls workbox-core workbox-precaching workbox-routing workbox-strategies workbox-expiration workbox-cacheable-response` shows every one reachable solely under workbox-build@7.4.1 — none is a direct dependency. scripts/build-sw.mjs:10 confirms esbuild resolves them from node_modules at prebuild time, so the build depends on npm hoisting behavior.
- **blast_radius:** scripts/build-sw.mjs, src/lib/pwa/sw-src.js, public/sw.js (prebuild step of every `npm run build`); breaks under pnpm/isolated-mode installs or if a future workbox-build release drops any of these from its dependency list
- **proposed_fix:** Add the six imported workbox-* packages to devDependencies at ^7.4.1 (matching the versions already installed and deduped against workbox-build) so the SW bundle's imports are declared, not inherited.
- **verification:** npm ls workbox-core workbox-precaching workbox-routing workbox-strategies workbox-expiration workbox-cacheable-response shows each as a direct devDependency; `npm run build:sw` produces a byte-identical public/sw.js (same versions dedupe).
- **disposition:** resolved — Stage 2 W5 (= PRI-008): the six workbox-* runtime packages (core/precaching/routing/strategies/expiration/cacheable-response) are declared as direct devDependencies at ^7.4.1, no longer relying on workbox-build's transitive hoist

### DEP-002 — Audit-gate exemption for GHSA-mh99-v99m-4gvg is stale — the advisory is no longer reported — and AGENTS.md still asserts 14 high entries that no longer exist

- severity `S3` · confidence `certain` · fix_class `AUTO-REVIEW` · design_impact `none`
- **evidence:** Live run of `npm run audit:check` prints: "GHSA-mh99-v99m-4gvg (brace-expansion) is no longer reported — drop this exemption." and "npm audit: 0 high, 0 critical across 0 distinct advisories." Baseline scratchpad audit.json independently shows vulnerabilities total 0 across 823 packages. scripts/check-audit.mjs:40-51 still carries the EXEMPT entry (plus verifyBraceExpansionPatched at lines 90-123), and AGENTS.md:83-97 still claims "the FULL-tree npm audit reports 14 high entries" and "npm audit cannot reach 0 on the full tree while this project uses ESLint 9" — both now false.
- **blast_radius:** scripts/check-audit.mjs, AGENTS.md, comment block in .github/workflows/ci.yml:62-65; no runtime effect
- **proposed_fix:** Follow the script's own designed prompt: remove the EXEMPT entry and its verify helper from scripts/check-audit.mjs (the gate then hard-fails if the advisory ever returns, which is the intended behavior), and update AGENTS.md:83-97 to record that the advisory was withdrawn/re-ranged and the per-major overrides are retained as defensive floors.
- **verification:** npm run audit:check exits 0 printing "0 high, 0 critical across 0 distinct advisories" with no "drop this exemption" line; grep -c GHSA-mh99 scripts/check-audit.mjs returns 0.
- **disposition:** resolved — Stage 2 W5: the stale GHSA-mh99-v99m-4gvg EXEMPT entry and its source-level verifier are removed from scripts/check-audit.mjs (now zero-exemption); AGENTS.md rewritten to record the advisory is no longer reported and the per-major overrides remain defensive floors

### DEP-003 — engines.node ">=20.0.0" understates the real floor — installed toolchain requires Node ^20.19.0 || >=22.12.0

- severity `S3` · confidence `certain` · fix_class `AUTO-REVIEW` · design_impact `none`
- **evidence:** package.json:8-10 declares engines.node ">=20.0.0". node_modules/vite/package.json engines: "^20.19.0 || >=22.12.0" (vite@7.3.6, hard dependency of vitest@4.1.10 and peer of @vitejs/plugin-react@5.2.0); sharp@0.35.3 engines ">=20.9.0"; glob@11.1.0 engines "20 || >=22" (excludes Node 21). A contributor on Node 20.0-20.18, 21.x, or 22.0-22.11 satisfies the project's engines field yet runs an unsupported vite. CI is unaffected (.github/workflows/ci.yml:28 pins node-version: 22).
- **blast_radius:** package.json engines field; local developer environments only — CI and Vercel (Node 22) are inside the supported range
- **proposed_fix:** Tighten engines.node to "^20.19.0 || >=22.12.0" (the intersection-defining constraint, from vite 7) so the declared floor matches what the toolchain actually supports.
- **verification:** node -e "const s=require('semver');['20.0.0','20.18.0','21.0.0','22.11.0'].forEach(v=>console.log(v, s.satisfies(v, require('./package.json').engines.node)))" prints false for all four; `npm install --dry-run` on Node 22 emits no EBADENGINE warnings.
- **disposition:** resolved — Stage 2 W5: engines.node tightened to '^20.19.0 || >=22.12.0' (the vite 7 intersection), so the declared floor matches what the toolchain actually supports

### DEP-004 — Four deprecated transitive packages in the installed tree: glob@11.1.0, source-map@0.8.0-beta.0, node-domexception@1.0.0, whatwg-encoding@3.1.1

- severity `S3` · confidence `certain` · fix_class `MANUAL-APPROVE` · design_impact `none`
- **evidence:** package-lock.json deprecated fields (extracted programmatically): node_modules/glob 11.1.0 "Old versions of glob are not supported..."; node_modules/source-map 0.8.0-beta.0 "work in this beta branch won't be included in future versions"; node_modules/node-domexception 1.0.0 "Use your platform's native DOMException"; node_modules/whatwg-encoding 3.1.1 "Use @exodus/bytes instead". npm ls traces: glob and source-map@0.8.0-beta.0 via workbox-build@7.4.1; node-domexception via openai@4.104.0 -> formdata-node@4.4.1; whatwg-encoding via jsdom@25.0.1 -> html-encoding-sniffer@4.0.0. None carries an open advisory (audit total = 0).
- **blast_radius:** Dev/build tree only, except node-domexception which sits in the production dependency graph via openai@4; no runtime defect today
- **proposed_fix:** No local fix for the workbox-build pair (upstream pins); clearing node-domexception requires the openai major bump (see separate finding); whatwg-encoding clears with a jsdom major bump (^25.0.1 declared, newer majors available). Track as upstream-currency debt rather than patching with overrides.
- **verification:** node -e over package-lock.json listing entries with a `deprecated` field returns empty (or only the accepted workbox-build residue) after the respective bumps; npm run test stays green.
- **disposition:** accepted — Stage 2 W5: the four deprecated transitives (glob@11, source-map beta, node-domexception, whatwg-encoding) carry no advisory; the workbox-build pair is upstream-pinned and node-domexception/whatwg-encoding clear only with the openai/jsdom major bumps. Tracked as upstream-currency debt (rides DEP-005), no override applied

### DEP-005 — openai pinned to legacy major 4 (installed 4.104.0), at least two majors behind current, dragging node-fetch@2 + whatwg-url@5 into the production dependency graph

- severity `S3` · confidence `likely` · fix_class `MANUAL-APPROVE` · design_impact `none`
- **evidence:** package.json:36 "openai": "^4.77.0"; npm ls shows openai@4.104.0 -> node-fetch@2.7.0 -> whatwg-url@5.0.0 plus @types/node-fetch@2.6.13/form-data and formdata-node -> node-domexception@1.0.0 (deprecated) — all in prod deps (baseline audit metadata: 82 prod packages). openai v5 (2025) moved to native fetch and dropped this chain; v6 is the current line as of the knowledge cutoff. The SDK is load-bearing across 9 files: src/lib/providers/{openai,openai-compat,mistral,xai,vision,config}.ts, src/lib/constants.ts, tests/unit/{models.test.tsx,vision-fallback.test.ts} (chat.completions-era v4 API).
- **blast_radius:** src/lib/providers/openai.ts, openai-compat.ts, mistral.ts, xai.ts, vision.ts, config.ts, src/lib/constants.ts, and the /api/enhance route paths that call them; upgrade is API-breaking (v4 -> v5+ changed client construction and streaming types)
- **proposed_fix:** Plan a deliberate openai v4 -> current-major migration for the compat providers (client init, streaming iterator types, error classes), which also removes deprecated node-domexception and the duplicate whatwg-url@5 from prod. Not urgent: zero open advisories today.
- **verification:** After migration: npm ls node-fetch whatwg-url shows no openai-rooted entries; npx vitest run tests/unit/compat-adapter.test.ts tests/unit/vision-fallback.test.ts tests/unit/models.test.tsx passes; npm audit --omit=dev --audit-level=high stays 0.
- **disposition:** deferred (by adjudication) — Stage 2 W5: openai v4→current is an API-breaking migration across 9 provider/test files (client init, streaming iterator types, error classes), not a hygiene commit; zero open advisories today. Left on the backlog with its evidence

## Track DEAD

> Track DEAD is largely clean at v0.3.0: madge (with tsconfig paths) reports zero orphan modules beyond App Router convention files and middleware; every component, hook, lib module, and store has a verified importer; the only comment runs over 10 lines (TransformationDiff.tsx:133-147, NewPromptFab.tsx:129-139) are explanatory prose, not disabled code; no stale feature flags exist in code — BRAND_MONOGRAMS_READY has been fully retired (survives only in CHANGELOG.md:2409 and tasks/lessons.md:245; Footer.tsx now inlines real monograms) and the sole live flag NEXT_PUBLIC_MEDIA_EXTRACTION is consumed (AttachmentTray.tsx:55) and documented; all 44 CSS custom properties across tokens.css/globals.css/dev-accents.css have at least one consumer (the twelve --dev-* accents resolve dynamically via var(--dev-${developer}) at LibraryBrowser.tsx:504); all 37 globals.css component classes are used; every .env.example variable is read by code; every runtime dependency has importers; knip/depcheck flags on sw-src.js, eslint, eslint-config-next, autoprefixer, and postcss are confirmed false positives (build-sw.mjs:27, eslint.config.mjs:70, postcss.config.mjs). The real dead surface concentrates in the PWA asset layer — two known carry-forwards: the entire public/splash directory (518,512 bytes, APPLE-01, still unwired) and 12 of 21 public/icons files (70,325 bytes) that no document references yet the SW precaches on every install (DISC-12) — plus the dead test:int npm script (DISC-01), three dead Tailwind config entries, and 22 unreachable exports (14 hand-verified module-internal symbols and 8 generated Supabase type aliases). One out-of-track observation passed along for the dependency track: sw-src.js imports six workbox-* packages that are only transitive dependencies of workbox-build (depcheck 'missing dependencies'), a hoisting hazard for the prebuild step, not dead code.

### DEAD-001 — 12 of 21 files in public/icons/ are referenced by no document, manifest, or metadata, yet all are precached by the service worker — 70,325 bytes (21.7% of the 324,404-byte icons dir) of unreachable assets downloaded on every SW install

- severity `S2` · confidence `certain` · fix_class `MANUAL-APPROVE` · design_impact `none` · prior_ref `DISC-12`
- **evidence:** manifest.webmanifest lists only icon-192/256/384/512/1024 + maskable-192/512; src/app/layout.tsx declares no icons metadata (App Router serves src/app/icon.png, icon.svg, apple-icon.png instead); grep -rEn 'icon-(48|72|96|128|144|152|167|180)' and 'favicon-' and 'icons/apple-touch-icon' across src, public, tests, scripts, docs matches nothing except public/sw.js. public/sw.js precache manifest enumerates all 21 icons (node scan of "url" entries) because scripts/build-sw.mjs:57 globs icons/**/*.png. Unreferenced set: apple-touch-icon.png (15985 B, iOS probes /apple-touch-icon.png at root, not /icons/), favicon-16/32/48 (4877 B), icon-48/72/96/128/144/152/167/180 (49463 B). Carry-forward of prior ledger DISC-12 (newlyDiscovered, unadjudicated).
- **blast_radius:** public/icons/** (12 of 21 files); scripts/build-sw.mjs precache glob; public/sw.js payload on every route for every installed client
- **proposed_fix:** Never delete the icons (protected path, dispositive). Ruling options: (a) narrow the build-sw.mjs precache glob to the manifest-referenced sizes, cutting 70,325 B from every install; or (b) wire the orphan sizes via layout metadata if they are meant to serve legacy devices. Either touches PWA offline surface — owner decision.
- **verification:** node -e scan of public/sw.js "url" entries after `npm run build:sw` lists only manifest/metadata-referenced assets; grep for the 12 filenames across src+public (excluding sw.js) still returns 0
- **disposition:** pending

### DEAD-002 — package.json `test:int` script targets tests/integration/ which does not exist — the script fails with 'No test files found, exiting with code 1' whenever invoked

- severity `S2` · confidence `certain` · fix_class `NEEDS-RULING` · design_impact `none` · prior_ref `DISC-01`
- **evidence:** package.json:21 "test:int": "vitest run --dir tests/integration"; ls tests/ shows only unit/, e2e/, stubs/, setup.ts, no integration/; executed `npx vitest run --dir tests/integration` -> 'No test files found, exiting with code 1'. Not wired into .github/workflows/ci.yml (grep 0 hits). Carry-forward of prior ledger DISC-01 (same wording at v0.2.1), still true at v0.3.0.
- **blast_radius:** package.json scripts block; any developer or future CI step invoking test:int; no runtime code affected
- **proposed_fix:** Prior ledger poses the exact fork: delete the script or implement tests/integration/. Deleting removes a documented entry point (CLAUDE.md gate wording says 'integration/e2e' but its command runs test:e2e only); implementing is net-new test scope. Ask, do not guess.
- **verification:** npx vitest run --dir tests/integration currently prints 'No test files found, exiting with code 1'; after ruling, either the script is gone from package.json or the directory exists with at least one passing spec
- **independently found by:** PRI ("npm run test:int still targets tests/integration, a directory that does not exist"); DOC ("AGENTS.md documents 'npm run test:int (integration only)' but the script exits 1 because tests/integration doe")
- **disposition:** resolved — Stage 2 W5 (ruling Q12=a): the test:int script is removed from package.json and its AGENTS.md doc line dropped — no integration tier is planned

### DEAD-003 — tailwind.config.ts carries dead entries: extend.backdropBlur.glass, extend.boxShadow.hair, extend.backgroundColor.glass generate utilities (backdrop-blur-glass, shadow-hair, bg-glass) used nowhere; colors.void-2 and colors.lift likewise emit no used utility

- severity `S3` · confidence `certain` · fix_class `AUTO-SAFE` · design_impact `none`
- **evidence:** grep -rn 'bg-glass|backdrop-blur-glass|shadow-hair' src tests = 0 hits; grep for void-2/-lift utility usage in src/app, src/components, src/stores = 0 hits; no dynamic class construction found (grep 'shadow-${|blur-${|bg-${' = 0). The glass effect is implemented directly by .glass in src/styles/globals.css:187-194 (literal backdrop-filter + var(--glass) + var(--glass-sheen)); --void-2 and --lift tokens themselves ARE alive via globals.css:673-674, so only the Tailwind mappings are dead. tailwind.config.ts lines 19-20 (void-2, lift), 89-96 (backdropBlur/boxShadow.hair/backgroundColor.glass). JIT emits nothing for unused keys, so zero runtime effect.
- **blast_radius:** tailwind.config.ts only; no emitted CSS changes anywhere
- **proposed_fix:** Remove extend.backdropBlur.glass, extend.boxShadow.hair, and extend.backgroundColor.glass. Keep colors.void-2 and colors.lift despite zero utility usage — they document the locked seven-role set (FINAL_PLAN section 2) and cost nothing under JIT.
- **verification:** npm run build after removal produces byte-identical CSS output; grep -rn 'bg-glass|backdrop-blur-glass|shadow-hair' src tests remains 0
- **disposition:** resolved — Stage 2 W4: dead tailwind entries removed (backdropBlur.glass, boxShadow.hair, backgroundColor.glass, colors.void-2, colors.lift) — the --void-2/--lift CSS vars stay for the mesh gradient
