## Track PERF

> PERF track on VIZION 0.3.0, baseline build green (exits.txt all 0). Route budget: 3 of 8 page routes exceed ~150 kB first-load JS — /enhance 221 kB, /profile 206 kB, /sign-in 176 kB — and the dominant heavy import is confirmed by chunk forensics to be supabase-js + gotrue (~62 kB gz across chunks 613-*/44530001-*, realtime/phoenix included) statically imported into interaction-only components (AttachmentTray, SettingsPanel, MediaManager). The neural mesh is well-engineered at rest (30fps cap, DPR<=2, viewport-scaled 18-54 nodes, hidden-pause, live reduced-motion handling, cheap halo instead of shadowBlur) but the explicit reduced-effects performance knob only CSS-hides the canvas while the rAF simulation (up to 1431 hypot pair-checks and ~1485 string allocations per frame on tablets) keeps running, and nothing pauses it under sheets or the keyboard where every blur(16px) glass panel re-composites per canvas frame. Streaming holds up: SSE deltas are rAF-batched, the 0.3.0 claim that the result tree reads the submitted snapshot (input+mode+target) is VERIFIED at EnhanceComposer.tsx:104-110/155 — but the unmemoized TransformationDiff still reconciles per keystroke, and each stream flush re-renders the whole composer subtree. Library: no virtualization, but scroll cost is genuinely bounded by content-visibility scroll-rows; the real gap is PromptRow's memo being defeated by per-render swipe callbacks (O(rows) reconciliation per search keystroke at 500 accumulated cards). Payload hygiene: SW precaches the full 320.8 KiB icon matrix (including the 115 KB icon-1024) on every first visit; no long-cache headers exist for /icons//splash//brand; all 8 font files preload on every route including mono weights the auth pages never render; 528 KB of splash PNGs deploy unreferenced (carry-forward APPLE-01, PR-5 becomes binding when wired). Explicit PASSes: zustand store uses per-slice selectors everywhere with a debounced (400ms, flush-on-hide) localStorage adapter; QueryProvider holds a stable client; ToastProvider memoizes its context value with stable children; ScrollStateManager stands glass blur down during scroll; no raw <img> anywhere (both images via next/image, auth hero SVG correctly sized 176x102 vs 1872x1084 viewBox with priority); fonts are self-hosted latin-subset woff2 with display:swap; the use-client census found no client entry components lacking interactivity (ScreenHeader/Footer/Wordmark/BrandPills are server components). Prior PERF-01 (no real-device measurement exists) remains open — every magnitude claim here is computed from source, not profiled.
>
| id | sev | conf | fix class | design | title |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| PERF-001 | S2 | certain | AUTO-REVIEW | none | supabase-js (~62 kB gz) is statically bundled into the /enhance and /profile first loads via interaction-only consumers, pushing both routes well past the 150 kB budget |
| PERF-002 | S2 | certain | AUTO-REVIEW | none | The reduced-effects performance knob hides the mesh canvas with CSS but never stops the rAF loop, so the full simulation and draw work keeps burning CPU into a display:none canvas at 30fps |
| PERF-003 | S2 | certain | AUTO-REVIEW | none | Typing in the composer re-renders the entire mounted result tree every keystroke — TransformationDiff is not memoized while EnhanceComposer subscribes to editorDraft, contradicting the R8 comment's claim |
| PERF-004 | S2 | certain | AUTO-REVIEW | none | PromptRow's memo is defeated by per-render callback identities in LibraryBrowser, so every keystroke in the library search re-renders all accumulated rows; the list is also fully rendered, never virtualized |
| PERF-005 | S2 | certain | AUTO-REVIEW | none | Service worker precaches the entire 320.8 KiB icon matrix — including the 115 KB icon-1024.png — on first visit for every browser user, installed or not |
| PERF-006 | S3 | certain | AUTO-REVIEW | none | Every SSE flush re-renders the whole EnhanceComposer subtree (pickers, rails, tray, keyboard bar), not just the streaming card |
| PERF-007 | S3 | certain | AUTO-REVIEW | none | All 8 font files (~150 KB) are preloaded on every page, including three JetBrains Mono weights used only in enhance/library output regions |
| PERF-008 | S3 | likely | AUTO-REVIEW | none | No long-lived Cache-Control headers for public static assets — icons, splash, and brand SVGs revalidate on every load (max-age=0), with only sw.js given explicit headers |

### PERF-001 — supabase-js (~62 kB gz) is statically bundled into the /enhance and /profile first loads via interaction-only consumers, pushing both routes well past the 150 kB budget

- severity `S2` · confidence `certain` · fix_class `AUTO-REVIEW` · design_impact `none`
- **evidence:** baseline/build.log:48,53 — /enhance First Load JS 221 kB, /profile 206 kB, /sign-in 176 kB (all other routes 103-132 kB). .next/app-build-manifest.json shows chunks 613-e0c981228efaf095.js (180.5 kB raw / 50 kB gz, contains supabase realtime+phoenix websocket markers) and 44530001-605b3917caa73239.js (60.3 kB raw / 12 kB gz, gotrue) in the /(app)/enhance/page, /(app)/profile/page and /(auth)/sign-in/page file lists only. Import chain: src/components/editor/EnhanceComposer.tsx:21 -> src/components/media/AttachmentTray.tsx:4 imports createClient from @/lib/supabase/client (createBrowserClient, src/lib/supabase/client.ts:3); runtime use is only inside event handlers (AttachmentTray.tsx:181,239,408,420,432). /profile pulls it via SettingsPanel.tsx:6 and MediaManager.tsx:4. next.config.ts:71 states nothing in src/ opens a realtime channel, yet RealtimeClient ships in the enhance-page critical path.
- **blast_radius:** src/components/media/AttachmentTray.tsx, src/components/settings/SettingsPanel.tsx, src/components/media/MediaManager.tsx, src/lib/supabase/client.ts; routes /enhance, /profile (and /sign-in to a lesser, arguably legitimate degree)
- **proposed_fix:** Replace the static createClient imports in AttachmentTray/SettingsPanel/MediaManager with a lazy accessor (const { createClient } = await import("@/lib/supabase/client")) invoked inside the handlers that already construct the client per call; keep type-only positions as `import type`. Cuts ~62 kB gz from /enhance and /profile first load, bringing /enhance to roughly the 150 kB line.
- **verification:** npm run build (allowed in fix phase) and confirm build.log route table shows /enhance and /profile first-load JS dropped by ~50-60 kB and chunks 613-*/44530001-* no longer appear in those pages' entries in .next/app-build-manifest.json; then exercise attach-file and settings save flows in e2e.
- **disposition:** pending

### PERF-002 — The reduced-effects performance knob hides the mesh canvas with CSS but never stops the rAF loop, so the full simulation and draw work keeps burning CPU into a display:none canvas at 30fps

- severity `S2` · confidence `certain` · fix_class `AUTO-REVIEW` · design_impact `none` · prior_ref `PERF-01`
- **evidence:** src/styles/globals.css:802-806 — [data-reduced-effects] .mesh-canvas { display: none; }. src/components/NeuralMeshBackground.tsx has no import of useUIStore and no observer for data-reduced-effects; the only stop conditions are prefers-reduced-motion (lines 205-216, 243) and document.hidden (218-226). step() (129-181) runs regardless: n = max(18, min(54, w*h/26000)) nodes (line 92) -> n(n-1)/2 pairwise Math.hypot calls per frame (153/frame on a 390x844 phone, 1431/frame on a 1024x1366 tablet), plus one template-string allocation per link stroke and per node fill (lines 150, 168, 172, 177 — worst case ~1485 short-lived strings/frame, ~44k/s at 30fps) drawn into a canvas the user explicitly asked to silence. The store comment (src/stores/ui.ts:99-101) defines reducedEffects as "a performance/comfort knob". Same gap: the loop also keeps running behind open portaled sheets and while the software keyboard is up, where every .glass panel (backdrop-filter: blur(16px), globals.css:190) re-blurs its backdrop on each of the 30 canvas invalidations per second.
- **blast_radius:** src/components/NeuralMeshBackground.tsx; every route (component mounts in the root layout, src/app/layout.tsx:70)
- **proposed_fix:** In NeuralMeshBackground's effect, also gate start/stop on the reduced-effects state — either subscribe to useUIStore(s => s.reducedEffects) or extend the existing MutationObserver (line 230) to watch data-reduced-effects on documentElement — calling stop() + clearRect when it is set and start() when cleared (mirroring the prefers-reduced-motion path). No visual change: the canvas is already hidden in that state.
- **verification:** npx vitest run tests/unit (mesh/reduced-effects specs) plus manual: toggle Reduced effects in Settings, confirm via DevTools Performance panel that no rAF ticks originate from NeuralMeshBackground while the attribute is set, and that the loop resumes on toggle-off.
- **disposition:** pending

### PERF-003 — Typing in the composer re-renders the entire mounted result tree every keystroke — TransformationDiff is not memoized while EnhanceComposer subscribes to editorDraft, contradicting the R8 comment's claim

- severity `S2` · confidence `certain` · fix_class `AUTO-REVIEW` · design_impact `none`
- **evidence:** src/components/editor/EnhanceComposer.tsx:66 subscribes to editorDraft (updates per keystroke, textarea onChange line 464); TransformationDiff is rendered as a child at lines 630-642 and src/components/diff/TransformationDiff.tsx:54 exports a plain function — no React.memo — so every keystroke reconciles its full tree: OutputSegments maps one <span> per diff segment (segments.tsx:82-97), plus the hunk review list, exporters strip and rationale cards. Its internal useMemos ([result]-keyed, lines 118-131, 159-166) avoid recompute but not reconciliation. The comment at EnhanceComposer.tsx:50-52 ("typing never re-renders the result (R8)") describes prop stability, not render behavior — props are stable (view.submitted snapshot, verified lines 104-110, 155, 175), which is exactly what makes React.memo effective here. On a long polish result (hundreds of diff segments) with a fast mobile typist this is per-keystroke O(segments) reconciliation stacked on the 30fps mesh.
- **blast_radius:** src/components/diff/TransformationDiff.tsx, src/components/editor/EnhanceComposer.tsx; /enhance route (also /library/[id] re-enhance consumer benefits)
- **proposed_fix:** Wrap TransformationDiff in React.memo (its props are already-stable snapshots plus stable-identity callbacks; hoist handleUse/handleRefine/handleAnswer into useCallback in EnhanceComposer so memo holds), and correct the R8 comment to say the result reads the submitted snapshot AND is memoized against composer re-renders.
- **verification:** npx vitest run tests/unit (result-view specs stay green); React DevTools Profiler: type in the prompt textarea with a result mounted and confirm TransformationDiff shows zero commits.
- **disposition:** pending

### PERF-004 — PromptRow's memo is defeated by per-render callback identities in LibraryBrowser, so every keystroke in the library search re-renders all accumulated rows; the list is also fully rendered, never virtualized

- severity `S2` · confidence `certain` · fix_class `AUTO-REVIEW` · design_impact `none`
- **evidence:** src/components/library/LibraryBrowser.tsx: PromptRow is memo-wrapped (line 455) but receives onFavorite={swipeFavorite} and onDelete={swipeDelete} (lines 245-246), both plain function declarations re-created on every LibraryBrowser render (lines 96-113, 116-121). setSearchDraft fires per keystroke (line 158), so at 500 prompts accumulated through Load more (extraCards, lines 79, 139 — unbounded client-side accumulation on top of keyset paging) each keystroke reconciles 500 PromptRow trees (~30+ elements each, plus a useSwipeActions hook instance per row). Paint cost is mitigated by .scroll-row content-visibility:auto / contain-intrinsic-size (globals.css:625-628), but React reconciliation remains O(rows) per keystroke and per any parent state change (sheetOpen, menuFor, cursor).
- **blast_radius:** src/components/library/LibraryBrowser.tsx; /library route at scale
- **proposed_fix:** Wrap swipeFavorite, swipeDelete (and loadMore/submitSearch while there) in useCallback with stable deps so the existing memo actually holds; the toast/router identities are stable. Virtualization proper (or a cap/flush of extraCards on filter change) is a larger follow-up if 500+ libraries become common — the stable-callback fix is the minimum viable change.
- **verification:** React DevTools Profiler on /library with 100+ rows: typing in the search field should commit only the search input subtree, zero PromptRow commits. npx vitest run tests/unit for library specs.
- **disposition:** pending

### PERF-005 — Service worker precaches the entire 320.8 KiB icon matrix — including the 115 KB icon-1024.png — on first visit for every browser user, installed or not

- severity `S2` · confidence `certain` · fix_class `AUTO-REVIEW` · design_impact `none`
- **evidence:** baseline/build.log:5 — "[build-sw] Precached 21 entries (320.8 KiB)". scripts/build-sw.mjs:57 globPatterns: ["icons/**/*.png", "manifest.webmanifest", "offline.html"] sweeps all 20 PNGs in public/icons (356K total, ls: icon-1024.png 115,333 B, icon-512 44,684 B, maskable-512 30,809 B, icon-384 28,736 B). The manifest (public/manifest.webmanifest) references only 192/256/384/512 + maskable variants; favicon-16/32/48, icon-48/72/96/128/144/152/167/180 and icon-1024 are browser-chrome/App-Store artifacts the SW never needs offline. precacheAndRoute (sw-src.js:65) downloads all of it in the install step on every first visit — ~130 KB of it (1024 + favicons + intermediate iOS sizes) serves no offline purpose, spent on mobile data before the user has enhanced a single prompt.
- **blast_radius:** scripts/build-sw.mjs, public/sw.js (built artifact); first-visit network cost on every route
- **proposed_fix:** Narrow globPatterns to the assets the offline experience actually uses: offline.html, manifest.webmanifest, icons/icon-192.png, icons/icon-256.png, icons/icon-384.png, icons/icon-512.png, icons/maskable-192.png, icons/maskable-512.png, icons/apple-touch-icon.png (~150 KiB). Remaining icons are still served on demand and runtime-cached by the StaleWhileRevalidate image route (sw-src.js:87-96).
- **verification:** node scripts/build-sw.mjs and confirm the log reports the reduced entry count/size; grep public/sw.js for icon-1024 (must be absent); e2e offline test (shell.spec.ts) stays green.
- **disposition:** pending

### PERF-006 — Every SSE flush re-renders the whole EnhanceComposer subtree (pickers, rails, tray, keyboard bar), not just the streaming card

- severity `S3` · confidence `certain` · fix_class `AUTO-REVIEW` · design_impact `none`
- **evidence:** src/lib/enhance/use-enhance.ts:82 keeps EnhanceStreamState in useState inside the hook consumed by EnhanceComposer (EnhanceComposer.tsx:95); delta flushes are rAF-batched (use-enhance.ts:88-111 — good), but each flush plus every status/thinking/usage event (lines 163, 166, 173) still setStates in the composer, reconciling ModeRig (memoized, cheap), TargetPicker/ThinkingPicker (unmemoized — TargetPicker.tsx:54 plain export), Segmented rails, AttachmentTray and KeyboardActionBar once per animation frame for the duration of the stream. Only StreamingResult's props actually change (EnhanceComposer.tsx:620-628). On low-end mobile this stacks with the 30fps mesh and per-frame glass re-blur during the highest-visibility interaction in the app.
- **blast_radius:** src/lib/enhance/use-enhance.ts, src/components/editor/EnhanceComposer.tsx; /enhance during active streams
- **proposed_fix:** Memoize the unmemoized composer children with stable callbacks (TargetPicker, ThinkingPicker, AttachmentTray, KeyboardActionBar), or move stream state out of the composer's render path (e.g., expose it via a subscription/ref consumed only by StreamingResult/StreamProgress) so per-frame commits touch only the streaming card.
- **verification:** React DevTools Profiler during a streamed enhance: per-flush commits should list only StreamingResult/StreamProgress. Existing streaming unit tests via npx vitest run stay green.
- **disposition:** pending

### PERF-007 — All 8 font files (~150 KB) are preloaded on every page, including three JetBrains Mono weights used only in enhance/library output regions

- severity `S3` · confidence `certain` · fix_class `AUTO-REVIEW` · design_impact `none`
- **evidence:** src/app/fonts/index.ts declares 8 woff2 sources across three localFont calls with display:"swap" and no preload override — next/font/local preloads every source by default, emitting 8 <link rel=preload as=font> on every route. Measured sizes: BebasNeue 13,768 B; RedditSans 400/500/600/700 = 17,016+18,144+18,128+18,140 B; JetBrainsMono 400/500/700 = 21,168+21,832+21,908 B (total 150,104 B). The mono family is used only in OUTPUT regions (fonts/index.ts:11 "enhanced-prompt OUTPUT region only"), which don't exist on /sign-in, /set-password, or /profile — ~65 KB of preload on the auth gate for glyphs that page never renders. Subsetting is done (latin, header comment line 5); swap is set; self-hosting is correct.
- **blast_radius:** src/app/fonts/index.ts; first-paint network contention on all routes, worst on auth/profile
- **proposed_fix:** Set preload: false on the jetBrainsMono localFont call (and consider dropping the 500 weight if unused — verify with a class-usage grep before removing). The family still loads on demand with swap when an output region first renders.
- **verification:** npm run build; view-source of a rendered auth page and count rel=preload font links (should drop from 8 to 5); confirm no FOUT regression on the enhance result card in e2e screenshots.
- **disposition:** pending

### PERF-008 — No long-lived Cache-Control headers for public static assets — icons, splash, and brand SVGs revalidate on every load (max-age=0), with only sw.js given explicit headers

- severity `S3` · confidence `likely` · fix_class `AUTO-REVIEW` · design_impact `none`
- **evidence:** next.config.ts:212-227 headers() sets security headers on /:path* and no-cache on /sw.js only; vercel.json:9-20 likewise covers only manifest content-type and sw.js. Nothing sets immutable/max-age for /icons/** (356K), /splash/** (528K), or /brand/*.svg, so Vercel/Next serve them at the default public, max-age=0, must-revalidate. Fonts are safe (next/font emits hashed /_next/static assets, immutable by default — src/app/fonts/index.ts). Mitigation exists for browsers with the SW installed (StaleWhileRevalidate image route, sw-src.js:87-96), but iOS fetches apple-touch-icon and any future startup images outside the SW, and the first-party revalidation chatter remains for all non-SW contexts.
- **blast_radius:** next.config.ts (or vercel.json); repeat-visit latency for /icons, /splash, /brand
- **proposed_fix:** Add a headers() rule for /icons/:path*, /splash/:path*, /brand/:path* with Cache-Control: public, max-age=31536000, immutable (assets are content-stable and regenerated only by scripts/generate-icons.mjs; if churn is a concern use max-age=86400, stale-while-revalidate=604800).
- **verification:** npm run build then curl -I the deployed /icons/icon-192.png and confirm the Cache-Control header; confirm sw.js still returns no-cache.
- **disposition:** pending

### DOC-004 — Eight vendored SIL OFL font files (Bebas Neue, Reddit Sans, JetBrains Mono woff2) are redistributed with no OFL license text or copyright notice anywhere in the repo; LICENSE is MIT-only

LICENSE:1-22 is pure MIT (c) 2026 Sean Vasey. - **proposed_fix:** Add src/app/fonts/OFL.txt (or one per family) carrying each family's original copyright line plus the OFL 1.1 text, and a one-line pointer in LICENSE or README that vendored fonts are OFL-licensed separately from the MIT code license.

- **disposition:** resolved — Stage 2 W5: src/app/fonts/OFL.txt carries the full SIL OFL 1.1 text with per-family copyright notices (Bebas Neue, Reddit Sans, JetBrains Mono); LICENSE, README, and fonts/index.ts point at it

- **evidence:** grep '^## \[' CHANGELOG.md: Unreleased at line 7, [0.3.0] - 2026-07-27 at line 1331 — 1,324 lines of unreleased content. docs/runbooks/release.md:8-9: minor for a new user-facing capability, patch for fixes. Discipline is otherwise good: 0.3.0 dated, all four releases dated, compare links present at CHANGELOG tail incl. [Unreleased] link.
- **blast_radius:** CHANGELOG.md, package.json version, release.yml trigger
- **proposed_fix:** Cut a release per docs/runbooks/release.md (bump version, date the section, add the compare link) once the current audit lands; a version bump is a release action requiring the owner.
- **verification:** CHANGELOG's Unreleased section shrinks to fresh work and a new dated section exists with a matching v-tag after merge
- **disposition:** pending

- **proposed_fix:** At capstone close, move the capstone artifacts under docs/audits/ (one directory), resolve the DOC-XXX placeholder to a real ledger id, and ensure 01-ledger.md exists before anything cites it. Do not move mid-run.

Origin: owner-directed work outside the Gate (Gemini non-JSON failure fix and
the owner console). Each item follows the standard ledger format; these feed
the same manual-approval review as the Stage 1 list.

| id | sev | conf | fix class | design | title |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| PRV-010 | S1 | certain | AUTO-REVIEW | none | Gemini SSE reader split frames on LF-LF while production sends CRLF — every run failed as "non-JSON response" |
| A11Y-012 | S2 | certain | AUTO-REVIEW | none | The reduced-effects switch in Settings has no accessible name (role="switch" with no aria-label; the Field label is an unassociated span) |

### PRV-010 — Gemini SSE reader split frames on LF-LF while production sends CRLF

- severity `S1` · confidence `certain` · fix_class `AUTO-REVIEW` · design_impact `none`
- **evidence:** Production screenshot (vizion-io.vercel.app v0.3.0): every Gemini run ended "The model returned a non-JSON response." Root cause: `src/lib/providers/google.ts` split SSE frames with `buf.indexOf("\n\n")`; the `alt=sse` endpoint delimits events with CRLF pairs, and `\r\n\r\n` contains no adjacent `\n\n` — zero frames parsed, empty assembly, guaranteed `parseEnhancePayload` failure (`formatters.ts:303`). The unit fixture (`tests/unit/google-adapter.test.ts`) authored LF-separated frames mirroring the implementation rather than the wire, so the suite stayed green over a total production failure.
- **blast_radius:** Gemini 3.6 Flash target (both thinking levels); every Gemini run since the adapter shipped
- **proposed_fix:** Accept either separator (`/\r?\n\r?\n/`), flush a final unterminated frame at stream close, and filter `thought: true` parts so reasoning can never corrupt the JSON envelope
- **verification:** `npx vitest run tests/unit/google-adapter.test.ts` (three regression tests added), plus a live Gemini run on the deployed preview
- **disposition:** **resolved** — shipped in PR #73 (`d573379`), 2026-08-01. Lesson recorded in `tasks/lessons.md` (wire-protocol fixtures must mirror the wire, not the implementation)

### A11Y-012 — The reduced-effects switch has no accessible name

- severity `S2` · confidence `certain` · fix_class `AUTO-REVIEW` · design_impact `none`
- **evidence:** `src/components/settings/SettingsPanel.tsx` (Appearance section): `<button role="switch" aria-checked=…>` with no `aria-label`; the visible "Reduced effects" label is a `<span>` inside `Field`, not programmatically associated (a `<button>` is not a labelable control for `<label htmlFor>`). Screen readers announce an unnamed switch (WCAG 4.1.2). Found while building the owner console, whose switch carries `aria-label="Open access"` for exactly this reason (`OwnerSection.tsx`).
- **blast_radius:** /profile Appearance section; one control
- **proposed_fix:** `aria-label="Reduced effects"` on the switch button (one attribute)
- **verification:** `getByRole("switch", { name: /reduced effects/i })` resolves in a unit test
- **disposition:** resolved — Stage 2 W3: aria-label on the reduced-effects switch (the Field caption is a sibling, not an association)

### SEC-011 — Closed-access boundary: server actions remain reachable for existing sessions

- severity `S3` · confidence `certain` · fix_class `NEEDS-RULING` · design_impact `none`
- **evidence:** By design, `open_access = false` gates: the authed shell (`(app)/layout.tsx` renders the closed notice), both model routes (403 before reserve — the spend-bearing surface), and new-account creation on the magic-link path. Not gated: server actions (library/drafts/profile reads and writes) invoked directly with a still-valid session cookie — RLS confines them to the caller's own rows, so there is no cross-user exposure and no provider spend, but a determined existing user could still sync their own data while "closed". The closed-screen copy ("Your data is safe") arguably implies exactly this.
- **blast_radius:** `src/lib/library/actions.ts`, `src/lib/drafts/actions.ts`, `src/lib/profile/actions.ts` while closed
- **proposed_fix:** Ruling: (a) accept the boundary as designed — closed access is a spend/registration control, not a data lockout (recommended; document in the ADR for the owner console), or (b) extend the `open_access` check into every server action (adds one settings read per action call)
- **verification:** If (b): a unit test per action asserting the closed-path refusal
- **disposition:** pending

### DSN-022 — First native range input (owner console accent slider)

- severity `S3` · confidence `certain` · fix_class `NEEDS-RULING` · design_impact `none`
- **evidence:** `OwnerSection.tsx` ships the app's first `<input type="range">`, styled only with `accent-[var(--accent-ink)]` (theme-correct ink — deliberately not raw laser, which is the 1.06:1 light-theme failure class). Native range styling varies by engine; the control predates any range recipe in the design system. DSN-015 (input recipes, ruling Q7) covered text inputs only.
- **blast_radius:** Owner section of /profile (owner-only surface)
- **proposed_fix:** Fold into the Q7 input-recipe ruling: bless the native accent-tinted range as the low-chrome recipe for owner-only surfaces, or specify a custom track/thumb treatment
- **verification:** Visual pass in both themes on the owner account
- **disposition:** pending
